##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\backend\app\analysis\backtest.py #####
"""
backtest.py — walk-forward backtest for the U1254 swing ruleset
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Answers the question a desk asks BEFORE trusting a mechanical system:
    "Does this ruleset actually have an edge — and how big?"

It reuses the project's OWN indicator math (technical.py: Wilder RSI,
EMA-seeded MACD, SMA) so the test reflects the real signals, not a
re-implementation. It walks each ticker's PriceHistory day by day,
recomputing indicators point-in-time (no look-ahead), enters when the
entry filter passes, and exits on stop / target / time-stop — net of
DSE transaction costs.

Outputs: trade count, win rate, avg win/loss, EXPECTANCY (the number that
matters), profit factor, max drawdown, avg hold. Plus a parameter sweep so
you can test whether RSI 50–68 actually beats other bands instead of
trusting it on faith.

USAGE (from the backend dir, where dse.db and technical.py live):
    python backtest.py                      # full backtest, default params
    python backtest.py --sweep rsi          # sweep the RSI entry band
    python backtest.py --tickers BXPHARMA,IDLC,SQURPHARMA

Self-test (no DB needed):
    python backtest.py --selftest
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import sqlite3, argparse, statistics, sys
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Optional

# Reuse the project's EXACT indicator functions. Run from the dir containing technical.py.
try:
    from technical import _sma, _rsi, _macd_series
except ImportError:
    # allow running the self-test standalone
    _sma = _rsi = _macd_series = None

DSE_ROUNDTRIP_COST = 0.012   # commission + 0.05% tax + slippage, round trip


# ── Strategy parameters (everything tunable — that's the point) ───────────────
@dataclass
class Params:
    rsi_min: float = 50.0
    rsi_max: float = 68.0
    require_macd_pos: bool = True
    require_above_sma20: bool = True
    require_above_sma50: bool = True
    vol_ratio_min: float = 1.3
    pct52_min: float = -22.0          # room-to-run window
    pct52_max: float = -2.0
    stop_atr_mult: float = None        # if set, stop = entry - mult*ATR; else use SMA20/pct
    stop_pct: float = 0.045            # fallback fixed stop distance
    target_R: float = 1.9              # target = entry + target_R * risk  (R-multiple)
    time_stop_days: int = 7
    warmup: int = 55                   # need >=50 for SMA50 + buffer
    cost: float = DSE_ROUNDTRIP_COST


@dataclass
class Trade:
    ticker: str
    entry_i: int
    entry_px: float
    exit_px: float
    bars_held: int
    exit_reason: str        # stop | target | time
    gross_ret: float
    net_ret: float          # after round-trip cost


# ── Indicator snapshot at a point in time (no look-ahead) ────────────────────
def _snapshot(closes, highs, lows, vols, i):
    """Compute the indicators the entry filter needs, using data UP TO day i only."""
    c = closes[: i + 1]
    if len(c) < 51:
        return None
    sma20 = _sma(c, 20)
    sma50 = _sma(c, 50)
    rsi = _rsi(c, 14)
    _macd, _sig, hist = _macd_series(c)
    avg_vol = sum(vols[max(0, i - 19): i + 1]) / min(20, i + 1) if vols else None
    vol_ratio = (vols[i] / avg_vol) if (avg_vol and avg_vol > 0) else None
    w52h = max(highs[max(0, i - 251): i + 1]) if highs else None
    pct52 = ((c[-1] - w52h) / w52h * 100) if w52h else None
    return dict(price=c[-1], sma20=sma20, sma50=sma50, rsi=rsi,
                macd_hist=hist, vol_ratio=vol_ratio, pct52=pct52)


def _entry_ok(s, p: Params) -> bool:
    if s is None or None in (s["sma20"], s["sma50"], s["rsi"], s["macd_hist"]):
        return False
    if not (p.rsi_min <= s["rsi"] <= p.rsi_max):                return False
    if p.require_macd_pos and s["macd_hist"] <= 0:             return False
    if p.require_above_sma20 and s["price"] <= s["sma20"]:     return False
    if p.require_above_sma50 and s["price"] <= s["sma50"]:     return False
    if p.vol_ratio_min and (s["vol_ratio"] or 0) < p.vol_ratio_min: return False
    if s["pct52"] is None or not (p.pct52_min <= s["pct52"] <= p.pct52_max): return False
    return True


def simulate_ticker(ticker, closes, highs, lows, vols, p: Params) -> list[Trade]:
    trades: list[Trade] = []
    i = p.warmup
    n = len(closes)
    while i < n - 1:
        s = _snapshot(closes, highs, lows, vols, i)
        if not _entry_ok(s, p):
            i += 1
            continue
        # Enter at NEXT bar's open ~ next close (no same-bar look-ahead)
        entry_i = i + 1
        entry_px = closes[entry_i]
        risk = entry_px * p.stop_pct
        stop_px = entry_px - risk
        target_px = entry_px + p.target_R * risk

        exit_px = exit_reason = None
        j = entry_i + 1
        while j < n:
            lo, hi = lows[j], highs[j]
            if lo <= stop_px:                       # stop first (conservative)
                exit_px, exit_reason = stop_px, "stop"; break
            if hi >= target_px:
                exit_px, exit_reason = target_px, "target"; break
            if (j - entry_i) >= p.time_stop_days:
                exit_px, exit_reason = closes[j], "time"; break
            j += 1
        if exit_px is None:                          # ran out of data
            exit_px, exit_reason, j = closes[-1], "eod", n - 1

        gross = (exit_px - entry_px) / entry_px
        net = gross - p.cost
        trades.append(Trade(ticker, entry_i, entry_px, exit_px, j - entry_i,
                            exit_reason, round(gross, 4), round(net, 4)))
        i = j + 1                                    # no overlapping trades per ticker
    return trades


# ── Aggregate metrics ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def summarize(trades: list[Trade]) -> dict:
    if not trades:
        return {"trades": 0}
    rets = [t.net_ret for t in trades]
    wins = [r for r in rets if r > 0]
    losses = [r for r in rets if r <= 0]
    gross_win = sum(wins)
    gross_loss = -sum(losses)
    # max drawdown on the sequential equity curve (1 unit compounded per trade)
    eq, peak, mdd = 1.0, 1.0, 0.0
    for r in rets:
        eq *= (1 + r)
        peak = max(peak, eq)
        mdd = min(mdd, eq / peak - 1)
    expectancy = statistics.mean(rets)
    return {
        "trades": len(trades),
        "win_rate": len(wins) / len(trades),
        "avg_win": statistics.mean(wins) if wins else 0.0,
        "avg_loss": statistics.mean(losses) if losses else 0.0,
        "expectancy": expectancy,                      # avg net return per trade
        "expectancy_per_100": expectancy * 100,
        "profit_factor": (gross_win / gross_loss) if gross_loss else float("inf"),
        "max_drawdown": mdd,
        "avg_bars": statistics.mean(t.bars_held for t in trades),
        "total_compounded": eq - 1,
        "exit_mix": {r: sum(1 for t in trades if t.exit_reason == r)
                     for r in ("stop", "target", "time", "eod")},
    }


def print_summary(title, m):
    print(f"\n── {title} ──")
    if m["trades"] == 0:
        print("  no trades triggered"); return
    print(f"  trades {m['trades']}  ·  win {m['win_rate']:.0%}  ·  "
          f"avg win {m['avg_win']*100:+.1f}%  avg loss {m['avg_loss']*100:+.1f}%")
    print(f"  EXPECTANCY {m['expectancy_per_100']:+.2f}% / trade  ·  "
          f"profit factor {m['profit_factor']:.2f}  ·  max DD {m['max_drawdown']*100:.1f}%")
    print(f"  avg hold {m['avg_bars']:.1f} bars  ·  exits {m['exit_mix']}")
    verdict = ("EDGE looks real" if m["expectancy"] > 0.003 and m["profit_factor"] > 1.3
               else "NO reliable edge — do not size up" if m["expectancy"] <= 0
               else "marginal — costs eat most of it")
    print(f"  → {verdict}")


# ── DB loading ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def load_ticker(con, ticker):
    q = ("SELECT date, COALESCE(close, ltp), high, low, volume "
         "FROM price_history WHERE ticker=? ORDER BY date ASC")
    rows = con.execute(q, (ticker,)).fetchall()
    closes = [float(r[1]) for r in rows if r[1] is not None]
    highs = [float(r[2]) if r[2] is not None else float(r[1]) for r in rows if r[1] is not None]
    lows = [float(r[3]) if r[3] is not None else float(r[1]) for r in rows if r[1] is not None]
    vols = [float(r[4]) if r[4] is not None else 0.0 for r in rows if r[1] is not None]
    return closes, highs, lows, vols


def run(db="dse.db", tickers=None, p: Params = None):
    p = p or Params()
    con = sqlite3.connect(db)
    if not tickers:
        tickers = [r[0] for r in con.execute(
            "SELECT DISTINCT ticker FROM price_history").fetchall()]
    all_trades = []
    for tk in tickers:
        closes, highs, lows, vols = load_ticker(con, tk)
        if len(closes) < p.warmup + 5:
            continue
        all_trades += simulate_ticker(tk, closes, highs, lows, vols, p)
    con.close()
    return all_trades


# ── Parameter sweep (kills the "RSI 50–68 is arbitrary" objection) ───────────
def sweep_rsi(db, tickers=None):
    print("\n=== RSI ENTRY-BAND SWEEP ===")
    for lo, hi in [(45, 65), (50, 68), (50, 72), (55, 70), (40, 60), (55, 75)]:
        p = Params(rsi_min=lo, rsi_max=hi)
        m = summarize(run(db, tickers, p))
        if m["trades"]:
            print(f"  RSI {lo}-{hi}:  n={m['trades']:4d}  win {m['win_rate']:.0%}  "
                  f"expectancy {m['expectancy_per_100']:+.2f}%  PF {m['profit_factor']:.2f}  "
                  f"maxDD {m['max_drawdown']*100:.0f}%")
        else:
            print(f"  RSI {lo}-{hi}:  no trades")


# ── Synthetic self-test (proves the engine works without the DB) ─────────────
def selftest():
    import math, random
    random.seed(7)
    # Build a trending series with noise so SOME entries trigger and resolve
    closes = []
    px = 100.0
    for t in range(400):
        drift = 0.004 * math.sin(t / 30)        # cyclical up/down regimes
        px *= (1 + drift + random.uniform(-0.012, 0.012))
        closes.append(round(px, 2))
    highs = [c * 1.012 for c in closes]
    lows = [c * 0.988 for c in closes]
    # occasional volume spikes so the vol_ratio filter can pass
    vols = [1000 * (3.0 if t % 11 == 0 else random.uniform(0.6, 1.2)) for t in range(len(closes))]
    if _sma is None:
        print("technical.py not importable — run from backend dir for the real test."); return
    # relaxed params for the self-test ONLY — just to exercise entries/exits/metrics
    p = Params(rsi_min=40, rsi_max=80, vol_ratio_min=1.2, pct52_min=-40, pct52_max=0)
    trades = simulate_ticker("SYNTH", closes, highs, lows, vols, p)
    print(f"Self-test: generated {len(trades)} trades on synthetic data.")
    print_summary("SELF-TEST (synthetic, not real edge)", summarize(trades))
    print("\nEngine OK. Point it at dse.db from the backend dir for the real backtest.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--db", default="dse.db")
    ap.add_argument("--tickers", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--sweep", default=None, choices=["rsi"])
    ap.add_argument("--selftest", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    tickers = a.tickers.split(",") if a.tickers else None

    if a.selftest:
        selftest()
    elif a.sweep == "rsi":
        sweep_rsi(a.db, tickers)
    else:
        trades = run(a.db, tickers)
        print_summary("FULL BACKTEST (default params)", summarize(trades))
        # exit-reason + per-ticker context helps sanity-check
        if trades:
            byt = {}
            for t in trades:
                byt.setdefault(t.ticker, []).append(t.net_ret)
            print("\n  per-ticker expectancy (top/bottom):")
            ranked = sorted(byt.items(), key=lambda kv: statistics.mean(kv[1]))
            for tk, rs in ranked[:3] + ranked[-3:]:
                print(f"    {tk:11} n={len(rs):3d}  exp {statistics.mean(rs)*100:+.2f}%")


##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\backend\app\analysis\fundamental.py #####
"""
app/analysis/fundamental.py
────────────────────────────
Fundamental analysis scoring for DSE stocks.
 
Inputs:  Fundamentals ORM row  +  current price from Stock row
Outputs: FundamentalResult with score (0-100) and BUY/HOLD/SELL signal
 
Scoring methodology (DSE-calibrated):
──────────────────────────────────────
  P/E ratio     — DSE market avg ~15x; below 10 is cheap, above 25 is expensive
  P/B ratio     — computed as LTP / NAV; below 1.5 is undervalued
  EPS           — positive = healthy; negative = loss-making, heavy penalty
  EPS growth    — requires two years of data; skipped if unavailable
  Dividend yield— bonus for income investors
  ROE           — return on equity if available
 
Each factor returns a score contribution (-20 to +20).
Combined score is normalised to 0-100.
score >= 62  → BUY
score 40-61  → HOLD
score <  40  → SELL
"""
 
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional
 
 
# ── DSE sector average P/E benchmarks ────────────────────────────────────────
# Used to judge whether a stock's P/E is cheap/fair/expensive vs its sector
SECTOR_PE_BENCHMARKS: dict[str, float] = {
    # Benchmarks derived from actual DSE data (profitable stocks, PE<50)
    "Bank":                          7.6,   # actual avg 7.61x
    "Banking":                       7.6,
    "Fuel and Power":                9.6,   # actual avg 9.55x
    "Fuel & Power":                  9.6,
    "Telecommunication":             12.9,  # actual avg 12.86x
    "Telecom":                       12.9,
    "Services and Real Estate":      13.3,  # actual avg 13.25x
    "Cement":                        14.1,  # actual avg 14.10x
    "Financial Institutions":        15.4,  # actual avg 15.36x
    "Non-Bank Financial":            15.4,
    "Textile":                       14.7,  # actual avg 14.65x (excl outliers)
    "Tannery Industries":             7.9,  # actual avg 7.85x
    "Insurance":                     17.1,  # actual avg 17.07x
    "Pharmaceuticals and Chemicals": 16.6,  # actual avg 16.64x
    "Pharmaceuticals":               16.6,
    "Food and Allied":               11.4,  # actual avg 11.43x
    "Food & Allied":                 11.4,
    "IT Sector":                     17.7,  # actual avg 17.67x
    "IT":                            17.7,
    "Jute":                          26.2,  # only 1 stock
    "Paper and Printing":            24.4,  # actual avg 24.41x
    "Paper & Printing":              24.4,
    "Travel and Leisure":             9.6,  # actual avg 9.63x
    "Engineering":                    9.9,  # actual avg 9.91x (excl outliers)
    "Ceramics Sector":               12.0,  # estimated
    "Miscellaneous":                 18.8,  # actual avg 18.83x
}
DEFAULT_MARKET_PE = 13.0   # actual DSE market average (profitable stocks, PE<50)
 
 
# ── Data containers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 
@dataclass
class FundamentalSignal:
    name:   str
    value:  str    # "Bullish" | "Neutral" | "Bearish"
    detail: str
    weight: float = 1.0
 
 
@dataclass
class FundamentalResult:
    ticker:          str
    current_price:   float
 
    # Raw values
    eps:             Optional[float] = None
    pe:              Optional[float] = None
    nav:             Optional[float] = None
    pb:              Optional[float] = None   # computed: price / nav
    dividend:        Optional[float] = None
    dividend_yield:  Optional[float] = None   # computed: dividend / price * 100
    roe:             Optional[float] = None
    paid_up_capital: Optional[float] = None
    sector:          Optional[str]   = None
 
    # Sector context
    sector_pe:       Optional[float] = None   # benchmark P/E for this sector
 
    # Output
    signals:         list[FundamentalSignal] = field(default_factory=list)
    score:           float = 50.0
    recommendation:  str   = "HOLD"
    warning:         Optional[str] = None
 
 


def _score_eps_trend(eps_history: dict) -> FundamentalSignal:
    years = sorted(eps_history.keys())
    if len(years) < 2:
        return None
    vals = [eps_history[y] for y in years if eps_history.get(y) is not None]
    if len(vals) < 2:
        return None
    latest = vals[-1]
    oldest = vals[0]
    if oldest <= 0:
        return None
    change_pct = (latest - oldest) / oldest * 100
    if change_pct >= 20:
        return FundamentalSignal("EPS Trend", "Bullish",
            f"EPS grew {change_pct:.0f}% over {len(years)} years", weight=2.0)
    elif change_pct >= 0:
        return FundamentalSignal("EPS Trend", "Neutral",
            f"EPS stable over {len(years)} years", weight=0.0)
    elif change_pct >= -30:
        return FundamentalSignal("EPS Trend", "Bearish",
            f"EPS declined {abs(change_pct):.0f}% over {len(years)} years", weight=1.5)
    else:
        return FundamentalSignal("EPS Trend", "Bearish",
            f"EPS collapsed {abs(change_pct):.0f}% over {len(years)} years", weight=3.0)



def _score_debt_equity(total_debt_mn: float, nav: float, shares: float) -> FundamentalSignal:
    """Score Debt/Equity ratio. High debt = major risk especially in high interest rate environment."""
    if not total_debt_mn or not nav or not shares or shares <= 0:
        return None
    equity_mn = (nav * shares) / 1_000_000
    if equity_mn <= 0:
        return None
    de = round(total_debt_mn / equity_mn, 2)
    if de < 0.3:
        return FundamentalSignal("Debt/Equity", "Bullish",
            f"D/E {de:.2f}x - very low leverage, financially conservative", weight=2.0)
    elif de < 0.8:
        return FundamentalSignal("Debt/Equity", "Bullish",
            f"D/E {de:.2f}x - manageable debt level", weight=1.0)
    elif de < 1.5:
        return FundamentalSignal("Debt/Equity", "Neutral",
            f"D/E {de:.2f}x - moderate leverage", weight=0.0)
    elif de < 2.5:
        return FundamentalSignal("Debt/Equity", "Bearish",
            f"D/E {de:.2f}x - elevated leverage, interest rate risk", weight=1.5)
    elif de < 5.0:
        return FundamentalSignal("Debt/Equity", "Bearish",
            f"D/E {de:.2f}x - high leverage, significant financial risk", weight=2.5)
    else:
        return FundamentalSignal("Debt/Equity", "Bearish",
            f"D/E {de:.2f}x - dangerous leverage level", weight=3.0)


# ── Individual factor scorers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
 
def _score_pe(pe: float, sector_pe: float) -> FundamentalSignal:
    """Score P/E ratio relative to sector benchmark."""
    ratio = pe / sector_pe   # < 1 = cheaper than sector, > 1 = more expensive
 
    if pe <= 0:
        return FundamentalSignal("P/E Ratio", "Bearish",
            f"Negative P/E ({pe:.1f}x) — company is loss-making", weight=3.0)
    elif ratio < 0.6:
        return FundamentalSignal("P/E Ratio", "Bullish",
            f"P/E {pe:.1f}x is well below sector avg {sector_pe:.1f}x — deeply undervalued",
            weight=3.0)
    elif ratio < 0.85:
        return FundamentalSignal("P/E Ratio", "Bullish",
            f"P/E {pe:.1f}x below sector avg {sector_pe:.1f}x — modestly undervalued",
            weight=1.5)
    elif ratio <= 1.15:
        return FundamentalSignal("P/E Ratio", "Neutral",
            f"P/E {pe:.1f}x in line with sector avg {sector_pe:.1f}x — fairly valued",
            weight=0.0)
    elif ratio <= 1.5:
        return FundamentalSignal("P/E Ratio", "Bearish",
            f"P/E {pe:.1f}x above sector avg {sector_pe:.1f}x — slight premium",
            weight=1.5)
    else:
        return FundamentalSignal("P/E Ratio", "Bearish",
            f"P/E {pe:.1f}x significantly above sector avg {sector_pe:.1f}x — expensive",
            weight=3.0)
 
 
def _score_pb(pb: float) -> FundamentalSignal:
    """Score Price-to-Book ratio."""
    if pb <= 0:
        return FundamentalSignal("P/B Ratio", "Bearish",
            f"Negative book value (P/B {pb:.2f}x) — equity erosion", weight=2.0)
    elif pb < 0.8:
        return FundamentalSignal("P/B Ratio", "Bullish",
            f"P/B {pb:.2f}x — trading below book value, strong value signal",
            weight=2.5)
    elif pb < 1.5:
        return FundamentalSignal("P/B Ratio", "Bullish",
            f"P/B {pb:.2f}x — trading near book value, reasonable",
            weight=1.0)
    elif pb < 3.0:
        return FundamentalSignal("P/B Ratio", "Neutral",
            f"P/B {pb:.2f}x — moderate premium to book", weight=0.0)
    else:
        return FundamentalSignal("P/B Ratio", "Bearish",
            f"P/B {pb:.2f}x — significant premium to book value", weight=1.5)
 
 
def _score_eps(eps: float) -> FundamentalSignal:
    """Score EPS — positive is good, negative is a red flag."""
    if eps > 20:
        return FundamentalSignal("EPS", "Bullish",
            f"Strong EPS ৳{eps:.2f} — high earnings power", weight=2.0)
    elif eps > 5:
        return FundamentalSignal("EPS", "Bullish",
            f"Healthy EPS ৳{eps:.2f}", weight=1.0)
    elif eps > 0:
        return FundamentalSignal("EPS", "Neutral",
            f"Positive but modest EPS ৳{eps:.2f}", weight=0.5)
    elif eps == 0:
        return FundamentalSignal("EPS", "Neutral",
            "EPS is zero — breakeven", weight=0.0)
    else:
        return FundamentalSignal("EPS", "Bearish",
            f"Negative EPS ৳{eps:.2f} — company is loss-making", weight=3.0)
 
 
def _score_dividend_yield(div_yield: float) -> FundamentalSignal:
    """Score dividend yield — DSE market avg is around 2-4%."""
    if div_yield >= 8:
        return FundamentalSignal("Dividend Yield", "Bullish",
            f"High yield {div_yield:.1f}% — strong income return", weight=1.5)
    elif div_yield >= 4:
        return FundamentalSignal("Dividend Yield", "Bullish",
            f"Good yield {div_yield:.1f}% — above market average", weight=1.0)
    elif div_yield >= 1:
        return FundamentalSignal("Dividend Yield", "Neutral",
            f"Modest yield {div_yield:.1f}%", weight=0.0)
    else:
        return FundamentalSignal("Dividend Yield", "Neutral",
            "No dividend or yield below 1%", weight=0.0)
 
 
def _score_nav_vs_price(price: float, nav: float) -> FundamentalSignal:
    """
    Separate NAV signal — focuses on margin of safety.
    Complements P/B by highlighting when price is dramatically below NAV.
    """
    if price < nav * 0.7:
        return FundamentalSignal("NAV Safety Margin", "Bullish",
            f"Price ৳{price} is 30%+ below NAV ৳{nav:.2f} — strong margin of safety",
            weight=2.0)
    elif price < nav:
        return FundamentalSignal("NAV Safety Margin", "Bullish",
            f"Price ৳{price} below NAV ৳{nav:.2f} — trading at discount",
            weight=1.0)
    elif price < nav * 1.5:
        return FundamentalSignal("NAV Safety Margin", "Neutral",
            f"Price ৳{price} within 50% of NAV ৳{nav:.2f} — reasonable premium",
            weight=0.0)
    else:
        return FundamentalSignal("NAV Safety Margin", "Bearish",
            f"Price ৳{price} is more than 50% above NAV ৳{nav:.2f}",
            weight=1.0)
 
 
# ── Main entry point ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 
def compute_fundamental_analysis(
    ticker:       str,
    current_price: float,
    eps:          Optional[float] = None,
    pe:           Optional[float] = None,
    nav:          Optional[float] = None,
    dividend:     Optional[float] = None,
    roe:          Optional[float] = None,
    paid_up:      Optional[float] = None,
    sector:       Optional[str]   = None,
    eps_history:  Optional[dict]  = None,
    total_debt_mn: Optional[float] = None,
    shares_outstanding: Optional[float] = None,
) -> FundamentalResult:
    """
    Score a stock's fundamentals and return a FundamentalResult.
 
    All parameters except ticker and current_price are optional —
    the scorer gracefully handles missing data and notes it in warnings.
    """
    result = FundamentalResult(
        ticker        = ticker.upper(),
        current_price = current_price,
        eps           = eps,
        pe            = pe,
        nav           = nav,
        dividend      = dividend,
        roe           = roe,
        paid_up_capital = paid_up,
        sector        = sector,
    )
 
    # Compute derived values
    if nav and nav > 0 and current_price > 0:
        result.pb = round(current_price / nav, 3)
 
    if dividend and dividend > 0 and current_price > 0:
        result.dividend_yield = round(dividend / current_price * 100, 2)
 
    sector_pe = SECTOR_PE_BENCHMARKS.get(sector, DEFAULT_MARKET_PE) if sector else DEFAULT_MARKET_PE
    result.sector_pe = sector_pe
 
    signals  = []
    missing  = []
 
    # ── EPS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if eps is not None:
        signals.append(_score_eps(eps))
    else:
        missing.append("EPS")
 
    # ── P/E ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if pe is not None:
        signals.append(_score_pe(pe, sector_pe))
    else:
        missing.append("P/E")
 
    # ── P/B and NAV ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if result.pb is not None:
        signals.append(_score_pb(result.pb))
        signals.append(_score_nav_vs_price(current_price, nav))
    else:
        missing.append("NAV/P/B")
 
    # ── Dividend yield ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if result.dividend_yield is not None:
        signals.append(_score_dividend_yield(result.dividend_yield))
 
    # ── ROE ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if roe is not None:
        if roe >= 20:
            signals.append(FundamentalSignal("ROE", "Bullish",
                f"Strong ROE {roe:.1f}% — management generating good returns", weight=1.5))
        elif roe >= 10:
            signals.append(FundamentalSignal("ROE", "Neutral",
                f"Adequate ROE {roe:.1f}%", weight=0.0))
        else:
            signals.append(FundamentalSignal("ROE", "Bearish",
                f"Low ROE {roe:.1f}% — poor capital efficiency", weight=1.0))
 
    # EPS Trend
    if eps_history:
        trend_sig = _score_eps_trend(eps_history)
        if trend_sig:
            signals.append(trend_sig)

    # D/E Ratio
    if total_debt_mn is not None and nav and shares_outstanding:
        de_signal = _score_debt_equity(total_debt_mn, nav, shares_outstanding)
        if de_signal:
            signals.append(de_signal)

    result.signals = signals
 
    # ── Score ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    bull = sum(s.weight for s in signals if s.value == "Bullish")
    bear = sum(s.weight for s in signals if s.value == "Bearish")
    maxi = sum(s.weight for s in signals if s.value != "Neutral")
 
    if maxi > 0:
        raw   = (bull - bear) / maxi    # -1 to +1
        score = round(50 + raw * 50, 1) # 0 to 100
    else:
        score = 50.0

    # Cap score based on signal count - sparse signals inflate scores
    active_signals = [s for s in signals if s.value != "Neutral"]
    if len(active_signals) < 2:
        score = min(score, 65.0)
    elif len(active_signals) < 3:
        score = min(score, 80.0)
 
    # Cap score if no EPS history available — can't fully trust without trend data
    if not eps_history and eps is not None and eps > 0:
        score = min(score, 85.0)  # good fundamentals but unverified trend

    # Penalize missing or negative earnings
    if eps is None and pe is None:
        score = min(score, 40.0)
    elif eps is not None and eps <= 0:
        score = min(score, 35.0)
    elif pe is None and eps is not None and eps > 0:
        score = min(score, 75.0)
    # Penalize extreme overvaluation
    elif pe is not None and pe > 100:
        score = min(score, 30.0)   # PE > 100x = speculative, never BUY
    elif pe is not None and pe > 50:
        score = min(score, 45.0)   # PE > 50x = expensive, max HOLD


    result.score = score
 
    # ── Recommendation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if score >= 62:
        result.recommendation = "BUY"
    elif score >= 42:
        result.recommendation = "HOLD"
    else:
        result.recommendation = "SELL"
 
    # ── Warning for missing data ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    if missing:
        result.warning = (
            f"Missing fundamental data: {', '.join(missing)}. "
            "Call POST /load-fundamentals to fetch from DSE."
        )
 
    return result
 


##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\backend\app\analysis\screener.py #####
"""
app/analysis/screener.py
────────────────────────
Stock screener — filters all DSE stocks by fundamental + technical
criteria and returns a ranked list.

Used by GET /screener endpoint in main.py.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from .. import models
from .technical import compute_technical_analysis
from .fundamental import compute_fundamental_analysis


@dataclass
class ScreenerResult:
    ticker:          str
    company:         Optional[str]
    sector:          Optional[str]
    ltp:             Optional[float]
    eps:             Optional[float]
    pe:              Optional[float]
    nav:             Optional[float]
    market_cap:      Optional[float]
    tech_score:      float
    fund_score:      float
    combined_score:  float
    recommendation:  str
    data_days:       int


def run_screener(
    db: Session,

    # Fundamental filters
    sector:          Optional[str]  = None,
    min_eps:         Optional[float] = None,
    max_eps:         Optional[float] = None,
    min_pe:          Optional[float] = None,
    max_pe:          Optional[float] = None,
    profitable_only: bool = False,   # eps > 0

    # Technical filters
    min_tech_score:  Optional[float] = None,
    max_tech_score:  Optional[float] = None,

    # Combined filters
    recommendation:  Optional[str]  = None,   # BUY | HOLD | SELL
    min_score:       Optional[float] = None,
    min_data_days:   int = 1,

    # Output
    limit:           int = 30,
    sort_by:         str = "combined_score",  # combined_score | tech_score | fund_score | pe | eps
    sort_dir:        str = "desc",

    # Weights
    tech_weight:     float = 0.45,
    fund_weight:     float = 0.55,
) -> list[ScreenerResult]:
    """
    Screen all stocks in the DB and return a ranked filtered list.

    Strategy:
      1. Pre-filter stocks by fundamental criteria using DB queries (fast)
      2. Load price history and run technical analysis (slower)
      3. Apply remaining filters and rank
    """

    # ── Step 1: DB-level fundamental pre-filter ───────────────────────────────
    stock_q = db.query(models.Stock)

    if sector:
        # Case-insensitive partial match
        stock_q = stock_q.filter(
            models.Stock.sector.ilike(f"%{sector}%")
        )

    stocks = stock_q.all()
    if not stocks:
        return []

    # Build fundamentals lookup
    tickers = [s.ticker for s in stocks]
    fund_map: dict[str, models.Fundamentals] = {
        f.ticker: f
        for f in db.query(models.Fundamentals)
        .filter(models.Fundamentals.ticker.in_(tickers))
        .all()
    }

    # Build price history lookup (fetch all at once to avoid N+1)
    from ..crud import get_price_history
    history_map: dict[str, list] = {}
    for ticker in tickers:
        history_map[ticker] = get_price_history(db, ticker, limit=200)

    # ── Step 2: Score and filter ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    tw = tech_weight / (tech_weight + fund_weight)
    fw = fund_weight / (tech_weight + fund_weight)

    results = []

    for stock in stocks:
        ticker   = stock.ticker
        fund_row = fund_map.get(ticker)
        history  = history_map.get(ticker, [])

        # Pre-filter before expensive scoring
        eps = fund_row.eps if fund_row else None
        pe  = fund_row.pe  if fund_row else None

        # Skip mutual funds and unknown sector stocks with no fundamental data
        if eps is None and pe is None and (stock.sector is None or stock.sector == "Unknown"):
            continue
        if profitable_only and (eps is None or eps <= 0):
            continue
        if min_eps is not None and (eps is None or eps < min_eps):
            continue
        if max_pe is not None and (pe is not None and pe > max_pe):
            continue
        if min_pe is not None and (pe is None or pe < min_pe):
            continue

        # Fundamental analysis
        eps_hist = {}
        if fund_row:
            for yr, attr in [(2021,"eps_2021"),(2022,"eps_2022"),(2023,"eps_2023"),(2024,"eps_2024"),(2025,"eps_2025")]:
                v = getattr(fund_row, attr, None)
                if v is not None:
                    eps_hist[yr] = v
        fund = compute_fundamental_analysis(
            ticker        = ticker,
            current_price = stock.ltp or stock.close or 0,
            eps           = fund_row.eps  if fund_row else None,
            pe            = fund_row.pe   if fund_row else None,
            nav           = fund_row.nav  if fund_row else None,
            dividend      = fund_row.dividend if fund_row else None,
            roe           = fund_row.roe      if fund_row else None,
            sector        = stock.sector,
            eps_history   = eps_hist if eps_hist else None,
            total_debt_mn = getattr(fund_row, "total_debt_mn", None) if fund_row else None,
            shares_outstanding = getattr(fund_row, "shares_outstanding", None) if fund_row else None,
        )

        # Technical analysis
        tech = compute_technical_analysis(ticker, history)

        # Combined score
        combined = round(tech.score * tw + fund.score * fw, 1)
        if combined >= 62:
            rec = "BUY"
        elif combined >= 42:
            rec = "HOLD"
        else:
            rec = "SELL"

        # ── Apply filters ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        if max_eps is not None and (eps is not None and eps > max_eps):
            continue
        if min_tech_score is not None and tech.score < min_tech_score:
            continue
        if max_tech_score is not None and tech.score > max_tech_score:
            continue
        if min_score is not None and combined < min_score:
            continue
        if recommendation and rec != recommendation.upper():
            continue
        if tech.data_days < min_data_days:
            continue

        results.append(ScreenerResult(
            ticker         = ticker,
            company        = stock.company,
            sector         = stock.sector,
            ltp            = stock.ltp or stock.close,
            eps            = eps,
            pe             = pe,
            nav            = fund_row.nav if fund_row else None,
            market_cap     = fund_row.market_cap if fund_row else None,
            tech_score     = round(tech.score, 1),
            fund_score     = round(fund.score, 1),
            combined_score = combined,
            recommendation = rec,
            data_days      = tech.data_days,
        ))

    # ── Step 3: Sort ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    sort_key_map = {
        "combined_score": lambda r: r.combined_score,
        "tech_score":     lambda r: r.tech_score,
        "fund_score":     lambda r: r.fund_score,
        "pe":             lambda r: r.pe if r.pe is not None else 9999,
        "eps":            lambda r: r.eps if r.eps is not None else -9999,
        "ltp":            lambda r: r.ltp if r.ltp is not None else 0,
    }
    key_fn  = sort_key_map.get(sort_by, sort_key_map["combined_score"])
    reverse = (sort_dir.lower() != "asc")
    results.sort(key=key_fn, reverse=reverse)

    return results[:limit]


##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\backend\app\analysis\technical.py #####
"""
app/analysis/technical.py
─────────────────────────
Pure-Python technical analysis — no pandas, no ta-lib dependency.
Takes a list of PriceHistory ORM rows and returns a TechnicalResult.
 
Indicators computed:
    SMA   — 20, 50, 200 day simple moving averages
    EMA   — 12, 26 day exponential moving averages
    MACD  — MACD line, signal line (9-day EMA of MACD), histogram
    RSI   — 14-period Wilder RSI
    BB    — 20-day Bollinger Bands (±2 std dev)
    VWAP  — Volume-weighted average price (rolling 20 days)
 
Scoring (0–100):
    Each indicator contributes a signal (Bullish / Neutral / Bearish)
    which maps to +weight / 0 / -weight.
    Final score is normalised to 0–100.
    Score >= 60  →  BUY
    Score 40–59  →  HOLD
    Score <  40  →  SELL
"""
 
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional
 
 
# ── Data containers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 
@dataclass
class IndicatorValues:
    """Raw computed values for every indicator."""
    # Price context
    current_price:  float
    prev_close:     Optional[float]
 
    # Moving averages
    sma20:   Optional[float] = None
    sma50:   Optional[float] = None
    sma200:  Optional[float] = None
    ema12:   Optional[float] = None
    ema26:   Optional[float] = None
 
    # MACD
    macd:         Optional[float] = None   # MACD line  = EMA12 - EMA26
    macd_signal:  Optional[float] = None   # 9-day EMA of MACD line
    macd_hist:    Optional[float] = None   # MACD - signal
 
    # RSI
    rsi: Optional[float] = None
 
    # Bollinger Bands
    bb_upper: Optional[float] = None
    bb_mid:   Optional[float] = None       # same as SMA20
    bb_lower: Optional[float] = None
    bb_width: Optional[float] = None       # (upper - lower) / mid  — volatility proxy
 
    # Volume
    avg_volume_20:  Optional[float] = None
    current_volume: Optional[float] = None
    volume_ratio:   Optional[float] = None  # current / avg  > 1 = above average
 
    # 52-week range (from available history)
    week52_high: Optional[float] = None
    week52_low:  Optional[float] = None
    pct_from_52h: Optional[float] = None   # (price - 52h) / 52h * 100
 
 
@dataclass
class Signal:
    name:      str
    value:     str          # "Bullish" | "Neutral" | "Bearish"
    detail:    str          # human-readable explanation
    weight:    float = 1.0  # how much this signal contributes to the score
 
 
@dataclass
class TechnicalResult:
    ticker:        str
    current_price: float
    indicators:    IndicatorValues
    signals:       list[Signal] = field(default_factory=list)
    score:         float  = 50.0   # 0–100
    recommendation: str   = "HOLD" # BUY | HOLD | SELL
    data_days:     int    = 0      # how many history rows were used
    warning:       Optional[str] = None  # set when data is insufficient
 
 
# ── Math helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 
def _sma(closes: list[float], n: int) -> Optional[float]:
    if len(closes) < n:
        return None
    return round(sum(closes[-n:]) / n, 4)
 
 
def _ema(closes: list[float], n: int) -> Optional[float]:
    """Standard EMA — seeds from the first n-period SMA."""
    if len(closes) < n:
        return None
    k   = 2 / (n + 1)
    val = sum(closes[:n]) / n          # seed: SMA of first n closes
    for price in closes[n:]:
        val = price * k + val * (1 - k)
    return round(val, 4)
 
 
def _ema_series(closes: list[float], n: int) -> list[float]:
    """Return the full EMA series (same length as closes, NaN-padded at start)."""
    if len(closes) < n:
        return []
    k      = 2 / (n + 1)
    result = [None] * (n - 1)
    val    = sum(closes[:n]) / n
    result.append(val)
    for price in closes[n:]:
        val = price * k + val * (1 - k)
        result.append(val)
    return result
 
 
def _rsi(closes: list[float], n: int = 14) -> Optional[float]:
    """Wilder RSI — uses Wilder smoothing (RMA), not simple EMA."""
    if len(closes) < n + 1:
        return None
 
    deltas = [closes[i] - closes[i - 1] for i in range(1, len(closes))]
    gains  = [max(d, 0.0) for d in deltas]
    losses = [abs(min(d, 0.0)) for d in deltas]
 
    # Seed with simple average of first n periods
    avg_gain = sum(gains[:n]) / n
    avg_loss = sum(losses[:n]) / n
 
    # Wilder smoothing for remaining periods
    for i in range(n, len(gains)):
        avg_gain = (avg_gain * (n - 1) + gains[i]) / n
        avg_loss = (avg_loss * (n - 1) + losses[i]) / n
 
    if avg_loss == 0:
        return 100.0
    rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
    return round(100 - (100 / (1 + rs)), 2)
 
 
def _bollinger(closes: list[float], n: int = 20, k: float = 2.0):
    """Returns (upper, mid, lower, width) or (None, None, None, None)."""
    if len(closes) < n:
        return None, None, None, None
    window = closes[-n:]
    mid    = sum(window) / n
    std    = (sum((c - mid) ** 2 for c in window) / n) ** 0.5
    upper  = round(mid + k * std, 4)
    lower  = round(mid - k * std, 4)
    mid    = round(mid, 4)
    width  = round((upper - lower) / mid, 4) if mid else None
    return upper, mid, lower, width
 
 
def _macd_series(closes: list[float]):
    """
    Returns (macd_line, signal_line, histogram) as scalars.
    macd_line   = EMA12 - EMA26
    signal_line = 9-day EMA of macd_line series
    """
    ema12_series = _ema_series(closes, 12)
    ema26_series = _ema_series(closes, 26)
 
    if not ema12_series or not ema26_series:
        return None, None, None
 
    # Align: both series start from index 25 (first valid EMA26 value)
    macd_line_series = []
    for e12, e26 in zip(ema12_series, ema26_series):
        if e12 is None or e26 is None:
            macd_line_series.append(None)
        else:
            macd_line_series.append(e12 - e26)
 
    valid_macd = [v for v in macd_line_series if v is not None]
    if len(valid_macd) < 9:
        macd_val = valid_macd[-1] if valid_macd else None
        return macd_val, None, None
 
    signal = _ema(valid_macd, 9)
    macd_val = valid_macd[-1]
    hist = round(macd_val - signal, 4) if signal is not None else None
    return round(macd_val, 4), signal, hist
 
 
def _vwap(closes: list[float], volumes: list[float], n: int = 20) -> Optional[float]:
    """Rolling VWAP over last n periods."""
    if len(closes) < n or len(volumes) < n:
        return None
    c = closes[-n:]
    v = volumes[-n:]
    total_vol = sum(v)
    if total_vol == 0:
        return None
    return round(sum(c[i] * v[i] for i in range(n)) / total_vol, 4)
 
 
# ── Signal builders ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 
def _trend_signals(ind: IndicatorValues) -> list[Signal]:
    signals = []
    p = ind.current_price
 
    if ind.sma20 is not None:
        if p > ind.sma20:
            signals.append(Signal("SMA20", "Bullish",
                f"Price ৳{p} above SMA20 ৳{ind.sma20}", weight=1.0))
        else:
            signals.append(Signal("SMA20", "Bearish",
                f"Price ৳{p} below SMA20 ৳{ind.sma20}", weight=1.0))
 
    if ind.sma50 is not None:
        if p > ind.sma50:
            signals.append(Signal("SMA50", "Bullish",
                f"Price ৳{p} above SMA50 ৳{ind.sma50}", weight=1.5))
        else:
            signals.append(Signal("SMA50", "Bearish",
                f"Price ৳{p} below SMA50 ৳{ind.sma50}", weight=1.5))
 
    if ind.sma200 is not None:
        if p > ind.sma200:
            signals.append(Signal("SMA200", "Bullish",
                f"Price above 200-day MA ৳{ind.sma200} — long-term uptrend", weight=2.0))
        else:
            signals.append(Signal("SMA200", "Bearish",
                f"Price below 200-day MA ৳{ind.sma200} — long-term downtrend", weight=2.0))
 
    # Golden / death cross
    if ind.sma20 and ind.sma50:
        if ind.sma20 > ind.sma50:
            signals.append(Signal("MA Cross", "Bullish",
                f"SMA20 ({ind.sma20}) above SMA50 ({ind.sma50}) — golden cross zone",
                weight=1.5))
        else:
            signals.append(Signal("MA Cross", "Bearish",
                f"SMA20 ({ind.sma20}) below SMA50 ({ind.sma50}) — death cross zone",
                weight=1.5))
 
    return signals
 
 
def _macd_signal(ind: IndicatorValues) -> list[Signal]:
    if ind.macd is None:
        return []
 
    signals = []
 
    if ind.macd_signal is not None:
        if ind.macd > ind.macd_signal:
            signals.append(Signal("MACD", "Bullish",
                f"MACD ({ind.macd:.3f}) above signal ({ind.macd_signal:.3f})",
                weight=2.0))
        else:
            signals.append(Signal("MACD", "Bearish",
                f"MACD ({ind.macd:.3f}) below signal ({ind.macd_signal:.3f})",
                weight=2.0))
 
    if ind.macd_hist is not None:
        if ind.macd_hist > 0.05:
            signals.append(Signal("MACD Histogram", "Bullish",
                f"Histogram positive ({ind.macd_hist:.3f}) — momentum building",
                weight=1.0))
        elif ind.macd_hist > 0:
            signals.append(Signal("MACD Histogram", "Neutral",
                f"Histogram barely positive ({ind.macd_hist:.3f}) — weak momentum",
                weight=0.0))
        elif ind.macd_hist < -0.05:
            signals.append(Signal("MACD Histogram", "Bearish",
                f"Histogram negative ({ind.macd_hist:.3f}) — momentum fading",
                weight=1.0))
        else:
            signals.append(Signal("MACD Histogram", "Neutral",
                f"Histogram near zero ({ind.macd_hist:.3f}) — no momentum",
                weight=0.0))
 
    # Zero-line cross
    if ind.macd > 0:
        signals.append(Signal("MACD Zero", "Bullish",
            "MACD above zero line", weight=0.5))
    else:
        signals.append(Signal("MACD Zero", "Bearish",
            "MACD below zero line", weight=0.5))
 
    return signals
 
 
def _rsi_signal(ind: IndicatorValues) -> list[Signal]:
    if ind.rsi is None:
        return []
 
    rsi = ind.rsi
    if rsi <= 30:
        return [Signal("RSI", "Bullish",
            f"RSI {rsi} — oversold, potential reversal upward", weight=2.5)]
    elif rsi <= 45:
        return [Signal("RSI", "Bullish",
            f"RSI {rsi} — approaching oversold, mild bullish bias", weight=1.0)]
    elif rsi <= 55:
        return [Signal("RSI", "Neutral",
            f"RSI {rsi} — neutral zone", weight=0.0)]
    elif rsi <= 70:
        return [Signal("RSI", "Bearish",
            f"RSI {rsi} — approaching overbought, mild bearish bias", weight=1.0)]
    else:
        return [Signal("RSI", "Bearish",
            f"RSI {rsi} — overbought, potential pullback", weight=2.5)]
 
 
def _bollinger_signal(ind: IndicatorValues) -> list[Signal]:
    if ind.bb_upper is None:
        return []
 
    p = ind.current_price
    signals = []
 
    if p < ind.bb_lower:
        signals.append(Signal("Bollinger", "Bullish",
            f"Price ৳{p} below lower band ৳{ind.bb_lower} — oversold squeeze",
            weight=2.0))
    elif p > ind.bb_upper:
        signals.append(Signal("Bollinger", "Bearish",
            f"Price ৳{p} above upper band ৳{ind.bb_upper} — overbought stretch",
            weight=2.0))
    else:
        # Position within band: 0 = at lower, 1 = at upper
        band_range = ind.bb_upper - ind.bb_lower
        position   = (p - ind.bb_lower) / band_range if band_range else 0.5
        if position < 0.35:
            signals.append(Signal("Bollinger", "Bullish",
                f"Price in lower third of Bollinger Band", weight=1.0))
        elif position > 0.65:
            signals.append(Signal("Bollinger", "Bearish",
                f"Price in upper third of Bollinger Band", weight=1.0))
        else:
            signals.append(Signal("Bollinger", "Neutral",
                f"Price mid-band — no directional bias", weight=0.0))
 
    return signals
 
 
def _volume_signal(ind: IndicatorValues) -> list[Signal]:
    if ind.volume_ratio is None:
        return []
 
    vr = ind.volume_ratio
    p  = ind.current_price
    pc = ind.prev_close
 
    if pc is None:
        return []
 
    price_up = p >= pc
 
    if vr >= 1.5 and price_up:
        return [Signal("Volume", "Bullish",
            f"Volume {vr:.1f}x average with price up — strong buying interest",
            weight=1.5)]
    elif vr >= 1.5 and not price_up:
        return [Signal("Volume", "Bearish",
            f"Volume {vr:.1f}x average with price down — strong selling pressure",
            weight=1.5)]
    elif vr < 0.5:
        return [Signal("Volume", "Neutral",
            f"Volume {vr:.1f}x average — low conviction move", weight=0.0)]
    else:
        return [Signal("Volume", "Neutral",
            f"Volume {vr:.1f}x average — normal activity", weight=0.0)]
 
 
def _week52_signal(ind: IndicatorValues) -> list[Signal]:
    if ind.pct_from_52h is None:
        return []
 
    pct = ind.pct_from_52h
    p   = ind.current_price
    w52l = ind.week52_low
 
    signals = []
 
    vol_ratio = ind.volume_ratio or 1.0

    if pct >= -3:
        # Very close to 52W high
        if vol_ratio >= 1.5:
            signals.append(Signal("52W Range", "Bullish",
                f"Within 3% of 52W high with {vol_ratio:.1f}x volume — breakout attempt", weight=1.5))
        elif vol_ratio >= 1.0:
            signals.append(Signal("52W Range", "Neutral",
                f"Near 52W high — watch for breakout confirmation", weight=0.0))
        else:
            signals.append(Signal("52W Range", "Bearish",
                f"Near 52W high with low volume — resistance likely", weight=0.5))
    elif pct >= -10:
        # Approaching 52W high
        if vol_ratio >= 1.2:
            signals.append(Signal("52W Range", "Bullish",
                f"{abs(pct):.1f}% below 52W high, volume building — momentum positive", weight=1.0))
    elif pct <= -40:
        signals.append(Signal("52W Range", "Bullish",
            f"More than 40% below 52-week high — deep value territory", weight=1.5))
    elif pct <= -25:
        signals.append(Signal("52W Range", "Neutral",
            f"{abs(pct):.1f}% below 52W high — significant recovery potential", weight=0.0))
 
    if w52l and p < w52l * 1.1:
        signals.append(Signal("52W Low", "Bullish",
            f"Price near 52-week low ৳{w52l} — contrarian opportunity", weight=1.0))
 
    return signals
 
 
# ── Main entry point ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 
def compute_technical_analysis(ticker: str, history_rows: list) -> TechnicalResult:
    """
    Takes a list of PriceHistory ORM objects (any order — will be sorted).
    Returns a fully populated TechnicalResult.
 
    Minimum data requirements:
        < 14 rows  → no RSI, no MACD, score unreliable  (warning issued)
        < 20 rows  → no Bollinger Bands
        < 26 rows  → no MACD
        < 50 rows  → no SMA50
        < 200 rows → no SMA200
    """
    if not history_rows:
        return TechnicalResult(
            ticker=ticker,
            current_price=0.0,
            indicators=IndicatorValues(current_price=0.0, prev_close=None),
            warning="No price history available. Call POST /load-dse first.",
        )
 
    # Sort oldest → newest
    rows = sorted(history_rows, key=lambda r: r.date)
 
    # Extract close prices — fall back to ltp when close is missing
    closes  = [float(r.close or r.ltp) for r in rows if (r.close or r.ltp) is not None]
    volumes = [float(r.volume) for r in rows if r.volume is not None]
 
    if not closes:
        return TechnicalResult(
            ticker=ticker,
            current_price=0.0,
            indicators=IndicatorValues(current_price=0.0, prev_close=None),
            warning="All price history rows have null close and ltp.",
        )
 
    current_price = closes[-1]
    prev_close    = closes[-2] if len(closes) >= 2 else None
    n             = len(closes)
 
    # ── Compute all indicators ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    sma20  = _sma(closes, 20)
    sma50  = _sma(closes, 50)
    sma200 = _sma(closes, 200)
    ema12  = _ema(closes, 12)
    ema26  = _ema(closes, 26)
 
    macd_val, macd_sig, macd_hist = _macd_series(closes)
    rsi_val = _rsi(closes, 14)
 
    bb_upper, bb_mid, bb_lower, bb_width = _bollinger(closes, 20)
 
    avg_vol    = sum(volumes[-20:]) / min(len(volumes), 20) if volumes else None
    curr_vol   = volumes[-1] if volumes else None
    vol_ratio  = round(curr_vol / avg_vol, 2) if (avg_vol and curr_vol and avg_vol > 0) else None
 
    highs  = [float(r.high) for r in rows if r.high is not None]
    lows   = [float(r.low)  for r in rows if r.low  is not None]
    w52h   = round(max(highs), 2) if highs else None
    w52l   = round(min(lows),  2) if lows  else None
    pct52h = round((current_price - w52h) / w52h * 100, 2) if w52h else None
 
    ind = IndicatorValues(
        current_price  = current_price,
        prev_close     = prev_close,
        sma20          = sma20,
        sma50          = sma50,
        sma200         = sma200,
        ema12          = ema12,
        ema26          = ema26,
        macd           = macd_val,
        macd_signal    = macd_sig,
        macd_hist      = macd_hist,
        rsi            = rsi_val,
        bb_upper       = bb_upper,
        bb_mid         = bb_mid,
        bb_lower       = bb_lower,
        bb_width       = bb_width,
        avg_volume_20  = round(avg_vol, 0) if avg_vol else None,
        current_volume = curr_vol,
        volume_ratio   = vol_ratio,
        week52_high    = w52h,
        week52_low     = w52l,
        pct_from_52h   = pct52h,
    )
 
    # ── Collect signals ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    signals = (
        _trend_signals(ind)
        + _macd_signal(ind)
        + _rsi_signal(ind)
        + _bollinger_signal(ind)
        + _volume_signal(ind)
        + _week52_signal(ind)
    )
 
    # ── Score (weighted) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Each signal contributes: Bullish = +weight, Neutral = 0, Bearish = -weight
    # Total possible = sum of all weights (all Bullish)
    # Normalise to 0–100 with 50 as neutral baseline
    bull_score = sum(s.weight for s in signals if s.value == "Bullish")
    bear_score = sum(s.weight for s in signals if s.value == "Bearish")
    max_score  = sum(s.weight for s in signals if s.value != "Neutral")
 
    if max_score > 0:
        raw   = (bull_score - bear_score) / max_score   # -1 to +1
        score = round(50 + raw * 50, 1)                 # 0 to 100
    else:
        score = 50.0

    # Momentum quality cap - prevents weak trends scoring 100
    vol_ratio  = ind.volume_ratio or 0
    macd_hist  = abs(ind.macd_hist or 0)
    if vol_ratio < 0.5 and macd_hist < 0.05:
        score = min(score, 75.0)   # low volume + flat MACD
    elif vol_ratio < 0.8 and macd_hist < 0.05:
        score = min(score, 85.0)   # below avg volume + flat MACD
    elif macd_hist < 0.02:
        score = min(score, 88.0)   # very flat MACD regardless of volume
 
    # ── Recommendation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if score >= 62:
        recommendation = "BUY"
    elif score >= 42:
        recommendation = "HOLD"
    else:
        recommendation = "SELL"
 
    # ── Warning for thin data ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    warning = None
    if n < 14:
        warning = (
            f"Only {n} days of history available — indicators are unreliable. "
            "Call POST /load-dse daily to build up history."
        )
    elif n < 26:
        warning = (
            f"{n} days of history — MACD unavailable, some signals missing. "
            "Accuracy improves after 26+ trading days."
        )
 
    return TechnicalResult(
        ticker         = ticker.upper(),
        current_price  = current_price,
        indicators     = ind,
        signals        = signals,
        score          = score,
        recommendation = recommendation,
        data_days      = n,
        warning        = warning,
    )

##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\backend\app\crud.py #####
import pandas as pd
from datetime import date, datetime
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from . import models


# ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def safe_float(value) -> float | None:
    """
    Coerce a value to float, handling DSE quirks:
      '2.90-0.10'  →  2.90   (merged cell artifact — take the first number)
      '1,234.50'   →  1234.5  (thousands separator)
      '-' / '--'   →  None   (DSE uses these for missing data)
    """
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return float(value)
    value = str(value).replace(",", "").strip()
    if value in ("-", "--", "N/A", ""):
        return None
    # '25.60-1.20' is a DSE rendering bug where two cells merge
    if "-" in value and not value.startswith("-"):
        value = value.split("-")[0]
    try:
        return float(value)
    except ValueError:
        return None


def _today() -> date:
    return datetime.utcnow().date()


# ── Stock CRUD ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def create_stock(
    db: Session,
    ticker: str,
    company: str | None = None,
    sector: str  | None = None,
) -> models.Stock:
    """
    Create a new Stock row or update company/sector on an existing one.
    Safe to call multiple times.
    """
    ticker = ticker.upper().strip()
    existing = db.query(models.Stock).filter(
        models.Stock.ticker == ticker
    ).first()

    if existing:
        if company:
            existing.company = company
        if sector:
            existing.sector = sector
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(existing)
        return existing

    stock = models.Stock(ticker=ticker, company=company, sector=sector)
    db.add(stock)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(stock)
    return stock


def get_stock(db: Session, ticker: str) -> models.Stock | None:
    return db.query(models.Stock).filter(
        models.Stock.ticker == ticker.upper()
    ).first()


def get_all_stocks(db: Session) -> list[models.Stock]:
    return db.query(models.Stock).order_by(models.Stock.ticker).all()


def delete_stock(db: Session, ticker: str) -> bool:
    stock = get_stock(db, ticker)
    if not stock:
        return False
    db.delete(stock)   # cascades to price_history via ondelete="CASCADE"
    db.commit()
    return True


# ── Price history queries ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def get_price_history(
    db: Session,
    ticker: str,
    limit: int = 200,
) -> list[models.PriceHistory]:
    """
    Return up to `limit` days of history for a ticker, newest first.
    200 days covers SMA-200. Pass limit=60 for faster indicator runs.
    """
    return (
        db.query(models.PriceHistory)
        .filter(models.PriceHistory.ticker == ticker.upper())
        .order_by(models.PriceHistory.date.desc())
        .limit(limit)
        .all()
    )


def get_price_history_range(
    db: Session,
    ticker: str,
    start: date,
    end: date,
) -> list[models.PriceHistory]:
    """Return history between two dates (inclusive), oldest first."""
    return (
        db.query(models.PriceHistory)
        .filter(
            models.PriceHistory.ticker == ticker.upper(),
            models.PriceHistory.date   >= start,
            models.PriceHistory.date   <= end,
        )
        .order_by(models.PriceHistory.date.asc())
        .all()
    )


# ── DSE bulk save ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def save_dse_data(df: pd.DataFrame, db: Session) -> dict:
    """
    Process a scraped DataFrame in a single transaction:

      1. Upsert the `stocks` table  — latest snapshot, always overwritten
      2. Upsert `price_history`     — one row per ticker per day, safe to
                                      call multiple times on the same day

    Returns:
        {
            "stocks_created":   int,
            "stocks_updated":   int,
            "history_inserted": int,
            "history_updated":  int,
            "skipped":          int,
        }
    """
    counts = {
        "stocks_created":   0,
        "stocks_updated":   0,
        "history_inserted": 0,
        "history_updated":  0,
        "skipped":          0,
    }

    today = _today()

    # ── 1. Load all existing Stock rows into memory (single query) ────────────
    existing_stocks: dict[str, models.Stock] = {
        s.ticker: s for s in db.query(models.Stock).all()
    }

    # ── 2. Load today's PriceHistory rows into memory (single query) ──────────
    # Knowing which tickers already have a row for today lets us decide
    # INSERT vs UPDATE without issuing a query per ticker.
    todays_history: dict[str, models.PriceHistory] = {
        h.ticker: h
        for h in db.query(models.PriceHistory)
        .filter(models.PriceHistory.date == today)
        .all()
    }

    # ── 3. Walk the DataFrame ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    for _, row in df.iterrows():
        ticker = str(row.get("ticker", "")).strip().upper()
        if not ticker:
            counts["skipped"] += 1
            continue

        ltp = safe_float(row.get("ltp"))
        if ltp is None:
            # A row with no price has no analysis value — skip it
            counts["skipped"] += 1
            continue

        # Build the shared field dict once (reused for both tables)
        fields = {
            "ltp":            ltp,
            "high":           safe_float(row.get("high")),
            "low":            safe_float(row.get("low")),
            "close":          safe_float(row.get("close")),
            "ycp":            safe_float(row.get("ycp")),
            "change":         safe_float(row.get("change")),
            "percent_change": safe_float(row.get("percent_change")),
            "trade":          safe_float(row.get("trade")),
            "value_mn":       safe_float(row.get("value_mn")),
            "volume":         safe_float(row.get("volume")),
        }

        # ── 3a. Upsert stocks table ───────────────────────────────────────────
        if ticker in existing_stocks:
            stock = existing_stocks[ticker]
            for field, val in fields.items():
                setattr(stock, field, val)
            counts["stocks_updated"] += 1
        else:
            stock = models.Stock(ticker=ticker, **fields)
            db.add(stock)
            existing_stocks[ticker] = stock   # prevent duplicate in same batch
            counts["stocks_created"] += 1

        # ── 3b. Upsert price_history for today ───────────────────────────────
        # `close` is the canonical price for technical indicators.
        # Fall back to `ltp` when close is missing (mid-session scrape).
        canonical_close = fields["close"] or ltp

        history_fields = {
            "open":           None,               # DSE doesn't publish open price
            "high":           fields["high"],
            "low":            fields["low"],
            "close":          canonical_close,
            "ltp":            ltp,
            "volume":         fields["volume"],
            "value_mn":       fields["value_mn"],
            "trade":          fields["trade"],
            "change":         fields["change"],
            "percent_change": fields["percent_change"],
        }

        if ticker in todays_history:
            # Row already exists for today — update in place
            history_row = todays_history[ticker]
            for field, val in history_fields.items():
                setattr(history_row, field, val)
            counts["history_updated"] += 1
        else:
            # First scrape of the day — insert a new row
            history_row = models.PriceHistory(
                ticker=ticker,
                date=today,
                **history_fields,
            )
            db.add(history_row)
            todays_history[ticker] = history_row  # prevent duplicate in same batch
            counts["history_inserted"] += 1

    # ── 4. Single commit covers both tables ───────────────────────────────────
    db.commit()
    return counts


# ── Fundamentals CRUD ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def get_fundamentals(db: Session, ticker: str) -> models.Fundamentals | None:
    return db.query(models.Fundamentals).filter(
        models.Fundamentals.ticker == ticker.upper()
    ).first()


def save_fundamentals(df, db: Session) -> dict:
    """
    Upsert fundamentals from amarstock DataFrame.
    Preserves NAV/dividend/roe if already set (not in amarstock data).
    Returns {updated, created, skipped}
    """
    counts = {"updated": 0, "created": 0, "skipped": 0}

    existing: dict[str, models.Fundamentals] = {
        f.ticker: f for f in db.query(models.Fundamentals).all()
    }

    for _, row in df.iterrows():
        ticker = str(row.get("ticker", "")).strip().upper()
        if not ticker:
            counts["skipped"] += 1
            continue

        eps = safe_float(row.get("eps"))
        pe  = safe_float(row.get("pe"))

        if eps is None and pe is None:
            counts["skipped"] += 1
            continue

        fields = {
            "eps":             eps,
            "pe":              pe,
            "q1_eps":          safe_float(row.get("q1_eps")),
            "q2_eps":          safe_float(row.get("q2_eps")),
            "q3_eps":          safe_float(row.get("q3_eps")),
            "q4_eps":          safe_float(row.get("q4_eps")),
            "annualized_eps":  safe_float(row.get("annualized_eps")),
            "market_cap":      safe_float(row.get("market_cap")),
            "shares_outstanding": safe_float(row.get("shares_outstanding")),
            "ltp_amarstock":   safe_float(row.get("ltp_amarstock")),
        }
        # Only overwrite sector on Stock if provided
        sector  = str(row.get("sector") or "").strip() or None

        if ticker in existing:
            fund = existing[ticker]
            for field, val in fields.items():
                setattr(fund, field, val)
            # Update sector on the Stock row too
            if sector:
                stock = db.query(models.Stock).filter(models.Stock.ticker == ticker).first()
                if stock and not stock.sector:
                    stock.sector = sector
            counts["updated"] += 1
        else:
            fund = models.Fundamentals(ticker=ticker, **fields)
            db.add(fund)
            existing[ticker] = fund
            if sector:
                stock = db.query(models.Stock).filter(models.Stock.ticker == ticker).first()
                if stock and not stock.sector:
                    stock.sector = sector
            counts["created"] += 1

    db.commit()
    return counts

    DATABASE_URL,
    connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}  # needed for SQLite + FastAPI
