<script>
const API = window.location.hostname === "localhost" ? "http://localhost:8000" : window.location.origin;
let allStocks = [];
let priceChart = null, volChart = null;
let currentTab = "market";

async function api(path) {
  try {
    const r = await fetch(API + path);
    return await r.json();
  } catch(e) { return null; }
}

function scoreColor(s) {
  if (s >= 62) return "#16a34a";
  if (s >= 42) return "#d97706";
  return "#dc2626";
}

function badge(rec) {
  return `<span class="badge ${rec}">${rec}</span>`;
}

function barCell(score) {
  const c = scoreColor(score);
  return `<div class="bar-wrap"><div class="bar-bg"><div class="bar-fill" style="width:${score}%;background:${c}"></div></div><span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:${c}">${score.toFixed(0)}</span></div>`;
}

// ── API Status ──
async function checkAPI() {
  const d = await api("/stocks?limit=1");
  const dot = document.getElementById("api-dot");
  const status = document.getElementById("api-status");
  const err = document.getElementById("api-error");
  if (d) {
    dot.className = "dot";
    status.textContent = "API connected";
    err.style.display = "none";
  } else {
    dot.className = "dot offline";
    status.textContent = "API offline";
    err.style.display = "block";
  }
}

// ── Navigation ──
function showTab(tab) {
  currentTab = tab;
  document.querySelectorAll(".nav button").forEach((b,i) => {
    b.className = ["briefing","market","screener","portfolio","controls","scan"][i] === tab ? "active" : "";
  });
  document.getElementById("view-briefing").style.display = tab === "briefing" ? "block" : "none";
  document.getElementById("view-controls").style.display = tab === "controls" ? "block" : "none";
  document.getElementById("view-scan").style.display = tab === "scan" ? "block" : "none";
  document.getElementById("view-market").style.display = tab === "market" ? "block" : "none";
  document.getElementById("view-screener").style.display = tab === "screener" ? "block" : "none";
  document.getElementById("view-portfolio").style.display = tab === "portfolio" ? "block" : "none";
  document.getElementById("view-detail").style.display = "none";
  if (tab === "briefing") loadBriefing();
  if (tab === "controls") loadDbStats();
  if (tab === "market" && allStocks.length === 0) loadMarket();
  if (tab === "screener") runScreener();
  if (tab === "portfolio") loadPortfolio();
}

function backToMarket() {
  document.getElementById("view-detail").style.display = "none";
  document.getElementById("view-" + currentTab).style.display = "block";
}

// ── Market ──
async function loadMarket() {
  const data = await api("/stocks");
  if (!data) return;
  allStocks = Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.items || data.stocks || []);
  renderGainersLosers();
  renderMarketTable(allStocks);
}

function renderGainersLosers() {
  const sorted = [...allStocks].sort((a,b) => (b.percent_change||0) - (a.percent_change||0));
  const gainers = sorted.slice(0,5);
  const losers = sorted.slice(-5).reverse();
  const el = document.getElementById("gainers-losers");
  el.innerHTML = `
    <div class="card-sm">
      <div class="section-title" style="color:#16a34a">▲ Top Gainers</div>
      ${gainers.map(s => `<div class="gainers-item" onclick="loadDetail('${s.ticker}')">
        <span class="ticker-link">${s.ticker}</span>
        <span>?${s.ltp?.toFixed(1)}</span>
        <span class="up">+${s.percent_change?.toFixed(2)}%</span>
      </div>`).join("")}
    </div>
    <div class="card-sm">
      <div class="section-title" style="color:#dc2626">▼ Top Losers</div>
      ${losers.map(s => `<div class="losers-item" onclick="loadDetail('${s.ticker}')">
        <span class="ticker-link">${s.ticker}</span>
        <span>?${s.ltp?.toFixed(1)}</span>
        <span class="dn">${s.percent_change?.toFixed(2)}%</span>
      </div>`).join("")}
    </div>
  `;
}

function renderMarketTable(stocks) {
  const tbody = document.getElementById("market-tbody");
  tbody.innerHTML = stocks.slice(0,150).map((s,i) => {
    const up = s.change >= 0;
    return `<tr class="${i%2===1?'alt':''}" onclick="loadDetail('${s.ticker}')">
      <td class="ticker-link">${s.ticker}</td>
      <td style="color:#6b7280">${(s.sector||"—").split(" ")[0]}</td>
      <td style="font-weight:600">?${s.ltp?.toFixed(1)}</td>
      <td class="${up?'up':'dn'}">${up?"+":""}${s.change?.toFixed(2)}</td>
      <td class="${up?'up':'dn'}">${up?"+":""}${s.percent_change?.toFixed(2)}%</td>
      <td>${s.volume?.toLocaleString()}</td>
      <td>${s.value_mn?.toFixed(2)}</td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join("");
}

function filterMarket() {
  const q = document.getElementById("market-search").value.toUpperCase();
  const filtered = allStocks.filter(s =>
    s.ticker.includes(q) || (s.sector||"").toUpperCase().includes(q)
  );
  renderMarketTable(filtered);
}

// ── Screener ──
async function runScreener() {
  const sector = document.getElementById("f-sector").value;
  const maxpe = document.getElementById("f-maxpe").value;
  const techscore = document.getElementById("f-techscore").value;
  const rec = document.getElementById("f-rec").value;
  const profitable = document.getElementById("f-profitable").checked;

  const params = new URLSearchParams({ limit: 30 });
  if (sector) params.set("sector", sector);
  if (maxpe) params.set("max_pe", maxpe);
  if (techscore) params.set("min_tech_score", techscore);
  if (rec) params.set("recommendation", rec);
  if (profitable) params.set("profitable_only", "true");

  const tbody = document.getElementById("screener-tbody");
  tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="9" style="text-align:center;color:#9ca3af;padding:30px">Scanning…</td></tr>`;

  const data = await api(`/screener?${params}`);
  if (!data || !data.results) {
    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="9" style="text-align:center;color:#dc2626;padding:30px">API error — is uvicorn running?</td></tr>`;
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = data.results.map((r,i) => `
    <tr class="${i%2===1?'alt':''}" onclick="loadDetail('${r.ticker}')">
      <td class="ticker-link">${r.ticker}</td>
      <td style="color:#6b7280">${(r.sector||"—").split(" ")[0]}</td>
      <td style="font-weight:600">?${r.ltp?.toFixed(1)}</td>
      <td class="${r.eps>0?'up':'dn'}">${r.eps?.toFixed(2)}</td>
      <td>${r.pe?.toFixed(1)}x</td>
      <td>${barCell(r.tech_score)}</td>
      <td>${barCell(r.fund_score)}</td>
      <td style="font-weight:700;color:${scoreColor(r.combined_score)}">${r.combined_score.toFixed(1)}</td>
      <td>${badge(r.recommendation)}</td>
    </tr>`).join("") || `<tr><td colspan="9" style="text-align:center;color:#9ca3af;padding:30px">No results</td></tr>`;
}

// ── Detail ──
async function loadDetail(ticker) {
  document.getElementById("view-market").style.display = "none";
  document.getElementById("view-screener").style.display = "none";
  document.getElementById("view-detail").style.display = "block";
  document.getElementById("d-ticker").textContent = ticker;
  document.getElementById("d-sector").textContent = "Loading…";
  document.getElementById("d-price").textContent = "…";
  document.getElementById("d-stats").innerHTML = "";
  document.getElementById("d-indicators").innerHTML = "";
  document.getElementById("d-signals").innerHTML = "";

  if (priceChart) { priceChart.destroy(); priceChart = null; }
  if (volChart) { volChart.destroy(); volChart = null; }

  const [stock, analysis, histRaw] = await Promise.all([
    api(`/stocks/${ticker}`),
    api(`/stocks/${ticker}/analysis`),
    api(`/stocks/${ticker}/history?limit=60`),
  ]);

  if (!stock) return;

  const up = stock.change >= 0;
  document.getElementById("d-sector").textContent = stock.sector || "—";
  document.getElementById("d-price").textContent = `?${stock.ltp?.toFixed(1)}`;
  document.getElementById("d-change").innerHTML = `<span style="color:${up?'#16a34a':'#dc2626'}">${up?"+":""}${stock.change?.toFixed(2)} (${stock.percent_change?.toFixed(2)}%)</span>`;
  document.getElementById("d-rec-badge").innerHTML = analysis ? badge(analysis.recommendation) : "";

  // Stats
  const ind = analysis?.technical?.indicators || {};
  const cs = analysis?.combined_score || 0;
  document.getElementById("d-stats").innerHTML = [
    ["Combined", cs.toFixed(1), scoreColor(cs)],
    ["Tech Score", analysis?.tech_score?.toFixed(1)||"—", scoreColor(analysis?.tech_score||0)],
    ["Fund Score", analysis?.fund_score?.toFixed(1)||"—", scoreColor(analysis?.fund_score||0)],
    ["RSI", ind.rsi?.toFixed(1)||"—", ind.rsi<30?"#16a34a":ind.rsi>70?"#dc2626":"#374151"],
    ["Volume", stock.volume?.toLocaleString()||"—", "#374151"],
    ["52W High", ind.week52_high?`?${ind.week52_high}`:"—", "#374151"],
  ].map(([l,v,c]) => `<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">${l}</div><div class="stat-value" style="color:${c}">${v}</div></div>`).join("");

  // History chart
  if (histRaw && histRaw.length > 0) {
    const hist = [...(histRaw?.history || histRaw || [])].reverse();
    const labels = hist.map(h => h.date?.slice(5) || "");
    const closes = hist.map(h => h.close);
    const volumes = hist.map(h => h.volume);

    priceChart = new Chart(document.getElementById("price-chart"), {
      type: "line",
      data: {
        labels,
        datasets: [
          { label: "Close", data: closes, borderColor: "#3b82f6", borderWidth: 2, fill: true,
            backgroundColor: "rgba(59,130,246,0.08)", pointRadius: 0, tension: 0.3 },
          ind.sma20 ? { label: "SMA20", data: Array(closes.length).fill(ind.sma20),
            borderColor: "#f59e0b", borderDash: [4,4], borderWidth: 1, pointRadius: 0, fill: false } : null,
          ind.sma50 ? { label: "SMA50", data: Array(closes.length).fill(ind.sma50),
            borderColor: "#ef4444", borderDash: [4,4], borderWidth: 1, pointRadius: 0, fill: false } : null,
        ].filter(Boolean)
      },
      options: {
        responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
        scales: {
          x: { ticks: { fontSize: 10, color: "#9ca3af", maxTicksLimit: 8 }, grid: { display: false } },
          y: { ticks: { color: "#9ca3af", callback: v => `?${v}` }, grid: { color: "#f3f4f6" } }
        }
      }
    });

    volChart = new Chart(document.getElementById("vol-chart"), {
      type: "bar",
      data: { labels, datasets: [{ label: "Volume", data: volumes, backgroundColor: "#93c5fd", borderRadius: 2 }] },
      options: {
        responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
        scales: {
          x: { ticks: { maxTicksLimit: 6, color: "#9ca3af" }, grid: { display: false } },
          y: { display: false }
        }
      }
    });
  }

  // Indicators
  const rows = [
    ["MACD", `${ind.macd?.toFixed(3)} / ${ind.macd_signal?.toFixed(3)}`],
    ["Bollinger", `${ind.bb_lower?.toFixed(1)} — ${ind.bb_upper?.toFixed(1)}`],
    ["SMA 20/50/200", `${ind.sma20?.toFixed(1)} / ${ind.sma50?.toFixed(1)} / ${ind.sma200?.toFixed(1)}`],
    ["EMA 12/26", `${ind.ema12?.toFixed(1)} / ${ind.ema26?.toFixed(1)}`],
    ["Volume ratio", `${ind.volume_ratio?.toFixed(2)}x avg`],
  ];
  if (analysis?.fundamental) {
    rows.push(["EPS", analysis.fundamental.eps?.toFixed(2)]);
    rows.push(["P/E", analysis.fundamental.pe?.toFixed(2) + "x"]);
    rows.push(["NAV", analysis.fundamental.nav || "—"]);
  }
  document.getElementById("d-indicators").innerHTML = rows.map(([k,v]) =>
    `<div class="ind-row"><span class="ind-key">${k}</span><span class="ind-val">${v}</span></div>`
  ).join("");

  // Signals
  const signals = analysis?.technical?.signals || [];
  document.getElementById("d-signals").innerHTML = signals.map(s => {
    let name="", value="", detail="";
    if (typeof s === "string") {
      name = (s.match(/name=([^;@}]+)/) || [])[1]?.trim() || "";
      value = (s.match(/value=([^;@}]+)/) || [])[1]?.trim() || "";
      detail = (s.match(/detail=([^;@}]+)/) || [])[1]?.trim() || "";
      detail = detail.replace(/[^\x00-\x7F]/g,"").trim();
    } else { name=s.name; value=s.value; detail=s.detail; }
    return `<div class="signal-row">
      <span class="signal-name">${name}</span>
      <span class="signal-badge ${value}">${value}</span>
      <span class="signal-detail">${detail}</span>
    </div>`;
  }).join("");
}

// ── Init ──

function loadPortfolio() {
  Promise.all([api('/portfolio'), api('/portfolio/analysis')]).then(function(res) {
    var holdings = res[0]; var analysis = res[1];
    var list = Array.isArray(holdings) ? holdings : (holdings && holdings.value ? holdings.value : []);
    var summary = analysis && analysis.summary ? analysis.summary : {};
    var plColor = (summary.total_pl||0) >= 0 ? '#16a34a' : '#dc2626';
    document.getElementById('p-summary').innerHTML = [
      ['Total Invested', 'BDT ' + (summary.total_invested||0).toLocaleString()],
      ['Current Value', 'BDT ' + (summary.total_value||0).toLocaleString()],
      ['Total P&L', 'BDT ' + ((summary.total_pl||0)>=0?'+':'') + (summary.total_pl||0).toLocaleString(), plColor],
      ['Return', ((summary.total_pl_pct||0)>=0?'+':'') + (summary.total_pl_pct||0).toFixed(2) + '%', plColor],
    ].map(function(x) { return '<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">'+x[0]+'</div><div class="stat-value" style="color:'+(x[2]||'#111827')+'">'+x[1]+'</div></div>'; }).join('');
    document.getElementById('p-tbody').innerHTML = list.map(function(h) {
      var pc = h.pl >= 0 ? '#16a34a' : '#dc2626';
      return '<tr><td class="ticker-link" onclick="loadDetail(\''+h.ticker+'\')" style="cursor:pointer">'+h.ticker+'</td><td style="color:#6b7280">'+(h.sector||'').split(' ')[0]+'</td><td>BDT '+h.buy_price.toFixed(1)+'</td><td><b>BDT '+h.current_price.toFixed(1)+'</b></td><td>'+h.invested.toLocaleString()+'</td><td>'+h.current_value.toLocaleString()+'</td><td style="color:'+pc+';font-weight:600">'+(h.pl>=0?'+':'')+h.pl.toLocaleString()+'</td><td style="color:'+pc+';font-weight:600">'+(h.pl_pct>=0?'+':'')+h.pl_pct.toFixed(2)+'%</td><td>'+badge(h.recommendation)+'</td><td><button onclick="removeHolding(\''+h.ticker+'\')" style="background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b;border:none;border-radius:4px;padding:4px 8px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px">Remove</button> <button onclick="openEdit(\'" +h.ticker+ "\'," +h.buy_price+ "," +h.quantity+ ")" style="background:#eff6ff;color:#1d4ed8;border:none;border-radius:4px;padding:4px 8px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px">Edit</button></td></tr>';
    }).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="10" style="text-align:center;color:#9ca3af;padding:30px">No holdings yet.</td></tr>';
    var opps = analysis && analysis.top_buy_opportunities ? analysis.top_buy_opportunities : [];
    if (opps.length) {
      document.getElementById('p-opportunities').innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:12px">Top Buy Opportunities</div><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px">'+opps.map(function(s){return '<div class="card-sm" onclick="loadDetail(\''+s.ticker+'\')" style="cursor:pointer;min-width:160px"><div style="font-weight:700;color:#1d4ed8">'+s.ticker+'</div><div style="color:#6b7280;font-size:12px">'+s.sector+'</div><div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:700">BDT '+s.ltp+'</div>'+badge(s.recommendation)+'</div>';}).join('')+'</div>';
    }
  });
}

function addHolding() {
  var ticker = document.getElementById('p-ticker').value.toUpperCase();
  var price = document.getElementById('p-price').value;
  var qty = document.getElementById('p-qty').value;
  if (!ticker || !price || !qty) { alert('Fill all fields'); return; }
  fetch('/portfolio?ticker='+ticker+'&buy_price='+price+'&quantity='+qty, {method:'POST'}).then(function(r){return r.json();}).then(function(d){alert(d.message||'Added');loadPortfolio();});
}

function removeHolding(ticker) {
  fetch('/portfolio/'+ticker, {method:'DELETE'}).then(function(){loadPortfolio();});
}


function loadBriefing() {
  var d = new Date();
  document.getElementById('briefing-date').textContent = d.toLocaleDateString('en-BD',{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'}) + ' � ' + d.toLocaleTimeString('en-BD',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
  document.getElementById('briefing-content').innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;color:#9ca3af;padding:60px">Loading market data...</div>';

  Promise.all([api('/stocks'), api('/screener?profitable_only=true&max_pe=15&limit=8&recommendation=BUY'), api('/portfolio/analysis')]).then(function(res) {
    var stocksRes = res[0]; var screenerRes = res[1]; var portfolioRes = res[2];
    if (!stocksRes) {
      document.getElementById('briefing-error').innerHTML = '<div class="error-box">Cannot connect to API. Start uvicorn first.</div>';
      document.getElementById('briefing-content').innerHTML = '';
      return;
    }
    document.getElementById('briefing-error').innerHTML = '';

    var stocks = stocksRes.stocks || [];
    var gainers = stocks.filter(function(s){return s.percent_change > 0;}).sort(function(a,b){return b.percent_change - a.percent_change;});
    var losers = stocks.filter(function(s){return s.percent_change < 0;}).sort(function(a,b){return a.percent_change - b.percent_change;});
    var unchanged = stocks.filter(function(s){return s.percent_change === 0;});
    var totalValue = stocks.reduce(function(s,x){return s+(x.value_mn||0);},0);
    var isBullish = gainers.length > losers.length;
    var screenerResults = screenerRes && screenerRes.results ? screenerRes.results : [];

    var html = '';

    // Market Overview
    html += '<div class="grid-4" style="margin-bottom:20px">';
    html += '<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Total Stocks</div><div class="stat-value">'+stocks.length+'</div></div>';
    html += '<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Advancing</div><div class="stat-value up">'+gainers.length+'</div></div>';
    html += '<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Declining</div><div class="stat-value dn">'+losers.length+'</div></div>';
    html += '<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Turnover</div><div class="stat-value">?'+totalValue.toFixed(0)+'mn</div></div>';
    html += '</div>';
    html += '<div style="margin-bottom:20px;font-size:13px;color:#6b7280">Market breadth: <span style="font-weight:600;color:'+(isBullish?'#16a34a':'#dc2626')+'">'+(isBullish?'Bullish':'Bearish')+'</span> &nbsp;�&nbsp; '+gainers.length+' up &nbsp;�&nbsp; '+losers.length+' down &nbsp;�&nbsp; '+unchanged.length+' flat</div>';

    // Gainers & Losers
    html += '<div class="grid-2" style="margin-bottom:20px">';
    html += '<div class="card"><div class="section-title" style="color:#16a34a">? Top Gainers</div>';
    gainers.slice(0,5).forEach(function(s){
      html += '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #f3f4f6;cursor:pointer" onclick="loadDetail(\''+s.ticker+'\')">';
      html += '<span class="ticker-link">'+s.ticker+'</span><span>?'+s.ltp.toFixed(1)+'</span><span class="up">+'+s.percent_change.toFixed(2)+'%</span></div>';
    });
    html += '</div>';
    html += '<div class="card"><div class="section-title" style="color:#dc2626">? Top Losers</div>';
    losers.slice(0,5).forEach(function(s){
      html += '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #f3f4f6;cursor:pointer" onclick="loadDetail(\''+s.ticker+'\')">';
      html += '<span class="ticker-link">'+s.ticker+'</span><span>?'+s.ltp.toFixed(1)+'</span><span class="dn">'+s.percent_change.toFixed(2)+'%</span></div>';
    });
    html += '</div></div>';

    // Buy Opportunities
    if (screenerResults.length) {
      html += '<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:20px"><div class="section-title">Top Buy Opportunities</div>';
      html += '<table class="tbl"><thead><tr><th>Ticker</th><th>Sector</th><th>Price</th><th>PE</th><th>P/B</th><th>Score</th><th>Signal</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
      screenerResults.forEach(function(s){
        var pb = s.nav && s.ltp ? (s.ltp/s.nav).toFixed(2)+'x' : '�';
        html += '<tr onclick="loadDetail(\'' + s.ticker + '\')">'; 
        html += '<td class="ticker-link">'+s.ticker+'</td>';
        html += '<td style="color:#6b7280">'+(s.sector||'').split(' ')[0]+'</td>';
        html += '<td style="font-weight:600">?'+(s.ltp||0).toFixed(1)+'</td>';
        html += '<td>'+(s.pe?s.pe.toFixed(1)+'x':'�')+'</td>';
        html += '<td>'+pb+'</td>';
        html += '<td style="font-weight:700;color:#1d4ed8">'+(s.combined_score||0).toFixed(1)+'</td>';
        html += '<td>'+badge(s.recommendation)+'</td></tr>';
      });
      html += '</tbody></table></div>';
    }

    // Portfolio
    if (portfolioRes && portfolioRes.summary) {
      var s = portfolioRes.summary;
      var pl = s.total_pl || 0;
      var pct = s.total_pl_pct || 0;
      var plColor = pl >= 0 ? '#16a34a' : '#dc2626';
      var holdings = (portfolioRes.hold||[]).concat(portfolioRes.buy_more||[]).concat(portfolioRes.sell_suggestions||[]);
      html += '<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:20px"><div class="section-title">Portfolio Status</div>';
      html += '<div class="grid-4" style="margin-bottom:16px">';
      html += '<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Invested</div><div class="stat-value">?'+s.total_invested.toLocaleString()+'</div></div>';
      html += '<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Current Value</div><div class="stat-value">?'+s.total_value.toLocaleString()+'</div></div>';
      html += '<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">P&L</div><div class="stat-value" style="color:'+plColor+'">'+(pl>=0?'+':'')+'?'+Math.abs(pl).toLocaleString()+'</div></div>';
      html += '<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Return</div><div class="stat-value" style="color:'+plColor+'">'+(pct>=0?'+':'')+pct.toFixed(2)+'%</div></div>';
      html += '</div>';
      html += '<table class="tbl"><thead><tr><th>Ticker</th><th>Buy</th><th>Current</th><th>P&L</th><th>%</th><th>Signal</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
      holdings.forEach(function(h){
        var pc = h.pl_pct||0;
        var c = pc>=0?'#16a34a':'#dc2626';
        html += '<tr onclick="loadDetail(\'' + h.ticker + '\')">'; 
        html += '<td class="ticker-link">'+h.ticker+'</td>';
        html += '<td>?'+h.buy_price.toFixed(1)+'</td>';
        html += '<td style="font-weight:600">?'+h.current_price.toFixed(1)+'</td>';
        html += '<td style="color:'+c+'">'+(h.pl>=0?'+':'')+'?'+(h.pl||0).toFixed(0)+'</td>';
        html += '<td style="color:'+c+'">'+(pc>=0?'+':'')+pc.toFixed(2)+'%</td>';
        html += '<td>'+badge(h.recommendation)+'</td></tr>';
      });
      html += '</tbody></table>';
      if ((portfolioRes.sell_suggestions||[]).length) {
        html += '<div style="margin-top:12px;padding:10px;background:#fee2e2;border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;color:#991b1b">Sell alert: '+(portfolioRes.sell_suggestions||[]).map(function(h){return h.ticker;}).join(', ')+'</div>';
      }
      html += '</div>';
    }

    // Action Plan
    html += '<div class="card"><div class="section-title">Today\'s Action Plan</div>';
    ['Check stop losses before market opens at 10:30 AM',
     'Avoid trading in first 30 min (volatile open)',
     'Best entry window: 11:00 AM � 12:30 PM',
     'Watch oversold stocks for RSI crossing back above 30',
     'Market closes 2:30 PM � Auto-update runs at 3:00 PM BST'
    ].forEach(function(t,i){
      html += '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #f3f4f6;font-size:13px"><span style="font-weight:600;color:#374151;min-width:16px">'+(i+1)+'</span><span style="color:#6b7280">'+t+'</span></div>';
    });
    html += '</div>';

    document.getElementById('briefing-content').innerHTML = html;
  });
}


var updateLog = [];

function logUpdate(msg) {
  var ts = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
  updateLog.unshift('[' + ts + '] ' + msg);
  if (updateLog.length > 20) updateLog.pop();
  var el = document.getElementById('update-log');
  if (el) el.innerHTML = updateLog.join('<br>');
}

function runTask(path, statusId) {
  var statusEl = document.getElementById(statusId);
  if (statusEl) statusEl.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#d97706">Running...</span>';
  logUpdate('Starting: ' + path);
  fetch('/'+path, {method:'POST', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(300000)})
    .then(function(r){
      if (!r.ok) { throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status); }
      return r.json();
    })
    .then(function(d){
      var msg = d.message || JSON.stringify(d).slice(0,100);
      if (statusEl) statusEl.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#16a34a">Done: ' + msg + '</span>';
      logUpdate('Done: ' + path + ' - ' + msg);
      loadDbStats();
    })
    .catch(function(e){
      if (statusEl) statusEl.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#dc2626">Error: ' + e.message + '</span>';
      logUpdate('Error: ' + path + ' - ' + e.message);
    });
}

function runAll() {
  var btn = document.getElementById('btn-all');
  var status = document.getElementById('all-status');
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.textContent = 'Running...';
  status.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#d97706">Step 1/3: Refreshing prices...</span>';
  logUpdate('Full refresh started');

  fetch('/load-dse', {method:'POST'}).then(function(r){return r.json();}).then(function(d){
    status.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#d97706">Step 2/3: Refreshing fundamentals...</span>';
    logUpdate('Prices done. Starting fundamentals...');
    return fetch('/load-fundamentals', {method:'POST'}).then(function(r){return r.json();});
  }).then(function(d){
    status.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#d97706">Step 3/3: Loading history...</span>';
    logUpdate('Fundamentals done. Starting history...');
    return fetch('/load-history?days=30', {method:'POST'}).then(function(r){return r.json();});
  }).then(function(d){
    status.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#16a34a">All updates complete!</span>';
    logUpdate('Full refresh complete');
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = 'Run All Updates';
    loadDbStats();
  }).catch(function(e){
    status.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#dc2626">Error: ' + e + '</span>';
    logUpdate('Full refresh error: ' + e);
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = 'Run All Updates';
  });
}

function loadDbStats() {
  fetch('/stocks').then(function(r){return r.json();}).then(function(d){
    var stocks = d.stocks || d || [];
    var updated = stocks.filter(function(s){return s.updated_at;});
    var latest = updated.length ? updated.sort(function(a,b){return b.updated_at > a.updated_at ? 1:-1;})[0].updated_at : 'N/A';
    var el = document.getElementById('db-stats');
    if (el) el.innerHTML =
      '<div class="grid-4">' +
      '<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Total Stocks</div><div class="stat-value">' + stocks.length + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Last Updated</div><div class="stat-value" style="font-size:13px">' + (latest ? latest.slice(0,16).replace('T',' ') : 'N/A') + '</div></div>' +
      '</div>';
  });
}


var editTicker = null;

function openEdit(ticker, buyPrice, qty) {
  editTicker = ticker;
  document.getElementById('edit-ticker-label').textContent = 'Editing: ' + ticker;
  document.getElementById('edit-price').value = buyPrice;
  document.getElementById('edit-qty').value = qty;
  document.getElementById('edit-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}

function closeEdit() {
  document.getElementById('edit-modal').style.display = 'none';
  editTicker = null;
}

function saveEdit() {
  var price = document.getElementById('edit-price').value;
  var qty = document.getElementById('edit-qty').value;
  if (!price || !qty) { alert('Fill all fields'); return; }
  fetch('/portfolio?ticker='+editTicker+'&buy_price='+price+'&quantity='+qty, {method:'POST'})
    .then(function(r){
      if (!r.ok) { alert('Error: ' + r.status); return null; }
      return r.json();
    })
    .then(function(d){
      if (!d) return;
      alert(d.message || 'Updated');
      closeEdit();
      loadPortfolio();
    })
    .catch(function(e){ alert('Error: ' + e); });
}

function confirmRemove() {
  if (confirm('Remove ' + editTicker + ' from portfolio?')) {
    fetch('/portfolio/'+editTicker, {method:'DELETE'})
      .then(function(){
        closeEdit();
        loadPortfolio();
      });
  }
}


var scanData = [];
var scanFilter = 'ALL';

function runScan() {
  document.getElementById('btn-scan').disabled = true;
  document.getElementById('btn-scan').textContent = 'Scanning...';
  document.getElementById('scan-progress').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('scan-results').innerHTML = '';
  document.getElementById('btn-copy').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('scan-claude-box').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('scan-filters').style.display = 'none';

  var bar = document.getElementById('scan-bar');
  var status = document.getElementById('scan-status');
  var pct = 0;
  var interval = setInterval(function() {
    pct = Math.min(pct + 2, 90);
    bar.style.width = pct + '%';
    if (pct < 30) status.textContent = 'Loading price data...';
    else if (pct < 60) status.textContent = 'Running technical analysis...';
    else status.textContent = 'Calculating scores...';
  }, 500);

  api('/screener?limit=200&sort_by=combined_score').then(function(data) {
    clearInterval(interval);
    bar.style.width = '100%';
    status.textContent = 'Scan complete!';
    setTimeout(function(){ document.getElementById('scan-progress').style.display = 'none'; }, 1000);

    scanData = data.results || [];
    document.getElementById('btn-scan').disabled = false;
    document.getElementById('btn-scan').textContent = 'Run Full Scan';
    document.getElementById('btn-copy').style.display = 'inline-block';
    document.getElementById('scan-filters').style.display = 'flex';
    filterScan('BUY');
  }).catch(function(e) {
    clearInterval(interval);
    document.getElementById('scan-progress').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('btn-scan').disabled = false;
    document.getElementById('btn-scan').textContent = 'Run Full Scan';
    document.getElementById('scan-error').innerHTML = '<div class="error-box">Scan failed: ' + e + '</div>';
  });
}

function filterScan(filter) {
  scanFilter = filter;
  ['ALL','BUY','HOLD','SELL','OVERSOLD','OVERBOUGHT'].forEach(function(f) {
    var btn = document.getElementById('f-'+f.toLowerCase());
    if (btn) btn.style.fontWeight = f === filter ? '700' : '400';
  });

  var filtered = scanData.filter(function(s) {
    if (filter === 'ALL') return true;
    if (filter === 'BUY') return s.recommendation === 'BUY';
    if (filter === 'HOLD') return s.recommendation === 'HOLD';
    if (filter === 'SELL') return s.recommendation === 'SELL';
    if (filter === 'OVERSOLD') return s.rsi && s.rsi < 30;
    if (filter === 'OVERBOUGHT') return s.rsi && s.rsi > 75;
    return true;
  });

  document.getElementById('scan-count').textContent = filtered.length + ' stocks';

  var html = '<table class="tbl"><thead><tr>';
  html += '<th>Ticker</th><th>Price</th><th>Score</th><th>Tech</th><th>Fund</th><th>PE</th><th>P/B</th><th>RSI</th><th>Sector</th><th>Signal</th>';
  html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';

  filtered.slice(0,50).forEach(function(s) {
    var pb = s.nav && s.ltp ? (s.ltp/s.nav).toFixed(2) : '-';
    var rsi = s.rsi ? s.rsi.toFixed(1) : '-';
    var rsiColor = s.rsi < 30 ? '#16a34a' : s.rsi > 75 ? '#dc2626' : '#374151';
    html += '<tr onclick="loadDetail(\''+s.ticker+'\')"><td class="ticker-link">'+s.ticker+'</td>';
    html += '<td>?'+(s.ltp||0).toFixed(1)+'</td>';
    html += '<td style="font-weight:700;color:#1d4ed8">'+(s.combined_score||0).toFixed(1)+'</td>';
    html += '<td>'+(s.tech_score||0).toFixed(1)+'</td>';
    html += '<td>'+(s.fund_score||0).toFixed(1)+'</td>';
    html += '<td>'+(s.pe?s.pe.toFixed(1)+'x':'-')+'</td>';
    html += '<td>'+pb+'</td>';
    html += '<td style="color:'+rsiColor+'">'+rsi+'</td>';
    html += '<td style="color:#6b7280;font-size:11px">'+(s.sector||'').split(' ')[0]+'</td>';
    html += '<td>'+badge(s.recommendation)+'</td></tr>';
  });

  html += '</tbody></table>';
  if (filtered.length > 50) html += '<div style="text-align:center;color:#6b7280;font-size:12px;padding:8px">Showing top 50 of '+filtered.length+' results</div>';
  document.getElementById('scan-results').innerHTML = html;
}

function copyScanForClaude() {
  var buys = scanData.filter(function(s){ return s.recommendation === 'BUY'; }).slice(0,15);
  var sells = scanData.filter(function(s){ return s.recommendation === 'SELL'; }).slice(0,10);
  var oversold = scanData.filter(function(s){ return s.rsi && s.rsi < 30; });
  var overbought = scanData.filter(function(s){ return s.rsi && s.rsi > 75; }).slice(0,8);

  var text = '=== DSE MARKET SCAN RESULTS ===\n';
  text += 'Date: ' + new Date().toLocaleDateString() + '\n';
  text += 'Total scanned: ' + scanData.length + ' stocks\n\n';

  text += '=== TOP BUY SIGNALS ===\n';
  text += 'Ticker       Score  Tech  Fund    PE    RSI  Sector\n';
  buys.forEach(function(s) {
    text += (s.ticker+'            ').slice(0,12) + ' ' +
            (s.combined_score||0).toFixed(1).padStart(5) + ' ' +
            (s.tech_score||0).toFixed(1).padStart(5) + ' ' +
            (s.fund_score||0).toFixed(1).padStart(5) + ' ' +
            (s.pe?s.pe.toFixed(1)+'x':'  -  ').padStart(6) + ' ' +
            (s.rsi?s.rsi.toFixed(1):'  -').padStart(5) + '  ' +
            (s.sector||'').slice(0,15) + '\n';
  });

  text += '\n=== TOP SELL SIGNALS ===\n';
  sells.forEach(function(s) {
    text += (s.ticker+'            ').slice(0,12) + ' Score=' + (s.combined_score||0).toFixed(1) + '\n';
  });

  text += '\n=== OVERSOLD RSI<30 ===\n';
  oversold.forEach(function(s) {
    text += (s.ticker+'            ').slice(0,12) + ' RSI=' + (s.rsi?s.rsi.toFixed(1):'-') + ' Score=' + (s.combined_score||0).toFixed(1) + '\n';
  });

  text += '\n=== OVERBOUGHT RSI>75 ===\n';
  overbought.forEach(function(s) {
    text += (s.ticker+'            ').slice(0,12) + ' RSI=' + (s.rsi?s.rsi.toFixed(1):'-') + ' Score=' + (s.combined_score||0).toFixed(1) + '\n';
  });

  text += '\n=== PORTFOLIO ===\n';
  text += 'Ask me to analyze this scan and recommend swing trades for the next 10 trading days.';

  document.getElementById('scan-claude-text').value = text;
  document.getElementById('scan-claude-box').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('scan-claude-box').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});
}


async function exportForClaude() {
  var btn = document.getElementById('btn-export');
  btn.textContent = 'Collecting...';
  btn.disabled = true;

  try {
    // Fetch all data in parallel
    var [stocksRes, buyRes, valueRes, sellRes, portfolioRes] = await Promise.all([
      api('/stocks'),
      api('/screener?profitable_only=true&limit=20&sort_by=combined_score'),
      api('/screener?profitable_only=true&max_pe=10&sort_by=fund_score&limit=10'),
      api('/screener?recommendation=SELL&limit=10&sort_by=combined_score&sort_dir=asc'),
      api('/portfolio/analysis'),
    ]);
    var buyResults = buyRes.results || [];
    var rsiMap = {};
    await Promise.all(buyResults.slice(0,20).map(async function(s) {
      try {
        var r = await api('/stocks/'+s.ticker+'/analysis/technical');
        if (r && r.indicators) rsiMap[s.ticker] = r.indicators.rsi;
      } catch(e) {}
    }));

    var stocks = stocksRes.stocks || [];
    var gainers = stocks.filter(function(s){return (s.percent_change||0)>0;}).sort(function(a,b){return b.percent_change-a.percent_change;});
    var losers = stocks.filter(function(s){return (s.percent_change||0)<0;}).sort(function(a,b){return a.percent_change-b.percent_change;});
    var totalValue = stocks.reduce(function(s,x){return s+(x.value_mn||0);},0);

    // Build sector summary from all stocks
    var sectorMap = {};
    stocks.forEach(function(s) {
      if (!s.sector) return;
      if (!sectorMap[s.sector]) sectorMap[s.sector] = {count:0, changes:[], value:0};
      sectorMap[s.sector].count++;
      if (s.percent_change) sectorMap[s.sector].changes.push(s.percent_change);
      sectorMap[s.sector].value += (s.value_mn||0);
    });
    var sectors = Object.keys(sectorMap).map(function(k) {
      var d = sectorMap[k];
      var avg = d.changes.length ? d.changes.reduce(function(a,b){return a+b;},0)/d.changes.length : 0;
      return {sector:k, stocks:d.count, avg_change:avg.toFixed(2), turnover_mn:d.value.toFixed(1)};
    }).sort(function(a,b){return b.avg_change-a.avg_change;});

    var port = portfolioRes || {};
    var summary = port.summary || {};
    var holdings = (port.hold||[]).concat(port.buy_more||[]).concat(port.sell_suggestions||[]);

    // Build report text
    var now = new Date().toLocaleString();
    var txt = '=== DSE ENGINE ANALYSIS EXPORT ===\n';
    txt += 'Generated: ' + now + '\n';
    txt += 'Last trading date: 2026-05-24 (pre-Eid)\n';
    txt += 'Market reopens: 2026-06-01\n\n';

    txt += '=== MARKET OVERVIEW ===\n';
    txt += 'Total stocks: ' + stocks.length + '\n';
    txt += 'Advancing: ' + gainers.length + ' | Declining: ' + losers.length + ' | Unchanged: ' + (stocks.length-gainers.length-losers.length) + '\n';
    txt += 'Breadth: ' + (gainers.length > losers.length ? 'BULLISH' : 'BEARISH') + '\n';
    txt += 'Total turnover: BDT ' + totalValue.toFixed(0) + ' mn\n\n';

    txt += '=== TOP 5 GAINERS ===\n';
    gainers.slice(0,5).forEach(function(s){
      txt += s.ticker.padEnd(12) + ' BDT ' + (s.ltp||0).toFixed(1).padStart(8) + '  +' + (s.percent_change||0).toFixed(2) + '%  Vol:' + (s.volume||0).toFixed(0) + '\n';
    });

    txt += '\n=== TOP 5 LOSERS ===\n';
    losers.slice(0,5).forEach(function(s){
      txt += s.ticker.padEnd(12) + ' BDT ' + (s.ltp||0).toFixed(1).padStart(8) + '  ' + (s.percent_change||0).toFixed(2) + '%  Vol:' + (s.volume||0).toFixed(0) + '\n';
    });

    txt += '\n=== SECTOR PERFORMANCE ===\n';
    txt += 'Sector                    Stocks  AvgChg%  Turnover(mn)\n';
    sectors.forEach(function(s){
      txt += s.sector.padEnd(26) + s.stocks.toString().padStart(5) + '  ' + s.avg_change.padStart(7) + '%  ' + s.turnover_mn + '\n';
    });

    txt += '\n=== TOP 20 BUY SIGNALS ===\n';
    txt += 'Ticker       Score  Tech  Fund    PE     PB   RSI  Sector\n';
    (buyRes.results||[]).forEach(function(s){
      var pb = s.nav && s.ltp ? (s.ltp/s.nav).toFixed(2) : '-';
      txt += s.ticker.padEnd(12) + ' ' +
             (s.combined_score||0).toFixed(1).padStart(5) + ' ' +
             (s.tech_score||0).toFixed(1).padStart(5) + ' ' +
             (s.fund_score||0).toFixed(1).padStart(5) + ' ' +
             (s.pe?s.pe.toFixed(1)+'x':'  -  ').padStart(6) + ' ' +
             pb.padStart(5) + '  ' +
             (rsiMap[s.ticker]?rsiMap[s.ticker].toFixed(1):'  -').padStart(4) + '  ' +
             (s.sector||'').slice(0,15) + '\n';
    });

    txt += '\n=== TOP VALUE STOCKS (PE<10) ===\n';
    txt += 'Ticker       Score    PE    PB   EPS    NAV\n';
    (valueRes.results||[]).forEach(function(s){
      var pb = s.nav && s.ltp ? (s.ltp/s.nav).toFixed(2) : '-';
      txt += s.ticker.padEnd(12) + ' ' +
             (s.combined_score||0).toFixed(1).padStart(5) + ' ' +
             (s.pe?s.pe.toFixed(1)+'x':'  -').padStart(6) + ' ' +
             pb.padStart(5) + ' ' +
             (s.eps?s.eps.toFixed(2):'  -').padStart(6) + ' ' +
             (s.nav?s.nav.toFixed(1):'  -').padStart(6) + '\n';
    });

    txt += '\n=== SELL/WEAK SIGNALS ===\n';
    (sellRes.results||[]).forEach(function(s){
      txt += s.ticker.padEnd(12) + ' Score=' + (s.combined_score||0).toFixed(1) + ' PE=' + (s.pe?s.pe.toFixed(1):'N/A') + '\n';
    });

    txt += '\n=== PORTFOLIO STATUS ===\n';
    txt += 'Total Invested: BDT ' + (summary.total_invested||0).toLocaleString() + '\n';
    txt += 'Current Value:  BDT ' + (summary.total_value||0).toLocaleString() + '\n';
    txt += 'P&L: BDT ' + (summary.total_pl||0).toLocaleString() + ' (' + (summary.total_pl_pct||0).toFixed(2) + '%)\n';
    txt += 'Avg Score: ' + (summary.avg_score||0).toFixed(1) + '/100\n\n';
    txt += 'Ticker       BuyPrice  Current     PL%  Score  Signal\n';
    holdings.forEach(function(h){
      txt += h.ticker.padEnd(12) + ' ' +
             h.buy_price.toFixed(1).padStart(8) + ' ' +
             h.current_price.toFixed(1).padStart(8) + ' ' +
             ((h.pl_pct>=0?'+':'')+h.pl_pct.toFixed(2)+'%').padStart(7) + ' ' +
             (h.score||0).toFixed(1).padStart(6) + '  ' +
             h.recommendation + '\n';
    });

    txt += '\n=== ANALYSIS REQUEST ===\n';
    txt += 'Please provide:\n';
    txt += '1. Overall market assessment\n';
    txt += '2. Top 3 swing trade opportunities for the next 10 days with entry/stop/target\n';
    txt += '3. Portfolio review - hold/add/exit recommendations\n';
    txt += '4. Key risks to watch\n';
    txt += '5. Sector rotation opportunities\n';

    // Show in textarea
    document.getElementById('scan-claude-text').value = txt;
    document.getElementById('scan-claude-box').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('scan-claude-box').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});

    btn.textContent = 'Export Full Analysis';
    btn.disabled = false;

  } catch(e) {
    btn.textContent = 'Export Full Analysis';
    btn.disabled = false;
    alert('Export failed: ' + e);
  }
}

checkAPI();
showTab("market");
</script>
<script src="/static/chart.min.js"></script></body>
</html>
















##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\backend\fix_api_url.py #####
content = open("dashboard.html", encoding="utf-8").read()

# Replace hardcoded localhost with dynamic host detection
old = 'const API = "http://localhost:8000";'
new = 'const API = window.location.hostname === "localhost" ? "http://localhost:8000" : window.location.origin;'

if old in content:
    content = content.replace(old, new)
    print("Fixed API URL to be dynamic")
else:
    print("Pattern not found")
    import re
    matches = re.findall(r'const API.*?;', content)
    print("Found:", matches[:3])

open("dashboard.html", "w", encoding="utf-8").write(content)

##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\backend\full_scan.py #####
"""full_scan.py � regenerates full_analysis.csv/.json with combined recommendations."""
import requests, csv, json, sqlite3

API = "http://localhost:8000"
DB  = "dse.db"

COLS = [
    "ticker", "sector", "ltp", "percent_change", "volume",
    "combined_score", "tech_score", "fund_score", "recommendation",
    "rsi", "macd_hist", "volume_ratio", "pct_from_52h", "sma20", "sma50",
    "eps", "pe", "nav", "pb", "eps_trend", "debt_equity",
]

def main():
    resp = requests.get(f"{API}/stocks", timeout=30).json()
    stock_list = resp.get("stocks", []) if isinstance(resp, dict) else resp
    universe = {s["ticker"]: s for s in stock_list if (s.get("ltp") or 0) > 0}
    print(f"Scanning {len(universe)} stocks...")

    trend_map, de_map = {}, {}
    try:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(DB)
        for t, tr, de in conn.execute("SELECT ticker, eps_trend, debt_equity FROM fundamentals"):
            trend_map[t] = tr
            de_map[t] = de
        conn.close()
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  (DB read skipped: {e})")

    rows, errors = [], 0
    for i, (ticker, s) in enumerate(universe.items(), 1):
        try:
            d = requests.get(f"{API}/stocks/{ticker}/analysis", timeout=15).json()
            ind = d.get("technical", {}).get("indicators", {})
            fund = d.get("fundamental", {})
            rows.append({
                "ticker": ticker,
                "sector": d.get("sector") or s.get("sector"),
                "ltp": d.get("current_price") or s.get("ltp"),
                "percent_change": s.get("percent_change"),
                "volume": s.get("volume"),
                "combined_score": d.get("combined_score"),
                "tech_score": d.get("tech_score"),
                "fund_score": d.get("fund_score"),
                "recommendation": d.get("recommendation"),
                "rsi": ind.get("rsi"),
                "macd_hist": ind.get("macd_hist"),
                "volume_ratio": ind.get("volume_ratio"),
                "pct_from_52h": ind.get("pct_from_52h"),
                "sma20": ind.get("sma20"),
                "sma50": ind.get("sma50"),
                "eps": fund.get("eps"),
                "pe": fund.get("pe"),
                "nav": fund.get("nav"),
                "pb": fund.get("pb"),
                "eps_trend": trend_map.get(ticker),
                "debt_equity": de_map.get(ticker),
            })
        except Exception:
            errors += 1
        if i % 50 == 0:
            print(f"  progress {i}/{len(universe)}")

    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["combined_score"] or 0, reverse=True)

    with open("full_analysis.csv", "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=COLS)
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(rows)
    with open("full_analysis.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(rows, f, indent=2)

    buys = [r for r in rows if r["recommendation"] == "BUY"]
    holds = sum(1 for r in rows if r["recommendation"] == "HOLD")
    sells = sum(1 for r in rows if r["recommendation"] == "SELL")
    print(f"\nDone. {len(rows)} stocks ({errors} errors) -> full_analysis.csv / full_analysis.json")
    print(f"BUY {len(buys)} | HOLD {holds} | SELL {sells}\n")
    print("Top 15 BUY recommendations:")
    for r in buys[:15]:
        print(f"  {r['ticker']:<12} {r['combined_score']:>5}  {r['sector']}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

    init_db()


##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\backend\morning_briefing.py #####
import requests
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime

API = "http://localhost:8000"

def get(path):
    try:
        return requests.get(API + path, timeout=10).json()
    except:
        return None

def divider(title=""):
    if title:
        print(f"\n{'='*20} {title} {'='*20}")
    else:
        print("="*60)

def fmt(n, prefix=""):
    if n is None: return "-"
    return f"{prefix}{n:,.2f}"

print()
print("+----------------------------------------------------------+")
print("�           DSE MORNING BRIEFING                          �")
print(f"�           {datetime.now().strftime('%A, %d %B %Y  %I:%M %p'):<46}�")
print("+----------------------------------------------------------+")

# -- 1. Market Overview ------------------------------------------
divider("MARKET OVERVIEW")
stocks = get("/stocks")
stock_list = stocks.get("stocks", []) if stocks else []

gainers = [s for s in stock_list if s.get("percent_change") and s["percent_change"] > 0]
losers  = [s for s in stock_list if s.get("percent_change") and s["percent_change"] < 0]
unchanged = [s for s in stock_list if s.get("percent_change") == 0]
total_value = sum(s.get("value_mn", 0) or 0 for s in stock_list)

print(f"  Total stocks    : {len(stock_list)}")
print(f"  Advancing       : {len(gainers)} stocks")
print(f"  Declining       : {len(losers)} stocks")
print(f"  Unchanged       : {len(unchanged)} stocks")
print(f"  Market breadth  : {'Bullish' if len(gainers) > len(losers) else 'Bearish'}")
print(f"  Total turnover  : BDT {total_value:,.1f} mn")

# -- 2. Top Gainers & Losers -------------------------------------
divider("TOP 5 GAINERS")
top_gainers = sorted(gainers, key=lambda x: x["percent_change"], reverse=True)[:5]
for s in top_gainers:
    print(f"  {s['ticker']:<12} BDT {s['ltp']:<8.1f} +{s['percent_change']:.2f}%  Vol: {s.get('volume',0):,.0f}")

divider("TOP 5 LOSERS")
top_losers = sorted(losers, key=lambda x: x["percent_change"])[:5]
for s in top_losers:
    print(f"  {s['ticker']:<12} BDT {s['ltp']:<8.1f} {s['percent_change']:.2f}%  Vol: {s.get('volume',0):,.0f}")

# -- 3. Screener Opportunities -----------------------------------
divider("TOP BUY OPPORTUNITIES")
screener = get("/screener?profitable_only=true&max_pe=15&limit=8&recommendation=BUY")
results = screener.get("results", []) if screener else []
print(f"  {'Ticker':<12} {'Price':>7} {'PE':>6} {'P/B':>5} {'Score':>6} {'Sector'}")
print(f"  {'-'*65}")
for s in results:
    nav = s.get("nav")
    ltp = s.get("ltp") or 0
    pb = round(ltp/nav, 2) if nav and nav > 0 else None
    print(f"  {s['ticker']:<12} {ltp:>7.1f} {s.get('pe') or 0:>6.1f} {str(pb or '-'):>5} {s.get('combined_score',0):>6.1f}  {(s.get('sector') or '')[:20]}")

# -- 4. Oversold Stocks ------------------------------------------
divider("OVERSOLD STOCKS (RSI < 30) - Potential Bounce")
conn = sqlite3.connect("dse.db")
cur = conn.cursor()
try:
    import json
    with open("tech_analysis_all.json") as f:
        tech = json.load(f)
    oversold = [t for t in tech if 0 < t.get("rsi", 100) < 30]
    oversold.sort(key=lambda x: x["rsi"])
    for t in oversold[:5]:
        cur.execute("SELECT ltp, sector FROM stocks WHERE ticker=?", (t["ticker"],))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        ltp = row[0] if row else 0
        sector = (row[1] or "")[:15] if row else ""
        rec = t.get("rec", "?")
        warning = " ? SELL-rated" if rec == "SELL" else ""
        print(f"  {t['ticker']:<12} RSI={t['rsi']:.1f}  Price=BDT {ltp:.1f}  Score={t.get('score',0):.0f}  Rec={rec}{warning}")
    if not oversold:
        print("  No stocks currently oversold")
except:
    print("  Run scan_all.py to get RSI data")

# -- 5. Overbought Warning ---------------------------------------
divider("OVERBOUGHT STOCKS (RSI > 75) - Avoid Chasing")
try:
    overbought = [t for t in tech if t.get("rsi", 0) > 75]
    overbought.sort(key=lambda x: x["rsi"], reverse=True)
    for t in overbought[:5]:
        cur.execute("SELECT ltp FROM stocks WHERE ticker=?", (t["ticker"],))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        ltp = row[0] if row else 0
        print(f"  {t['ticker']:<12} RSI={t['rsi']:.1f}  Price=BDT {ltp:.1f}  Score={t.get('score',0):.0f}")
except:
    print("  Run scan_all.py to get RSI data")

# -- 6. Portfolio Status -----------------------------------------
divider("PORTFOLIO STATUS")
portfolio = get("/portfolio/analysis")
if portfolio and "summary" in portfolio:
    s = portfolio["summary"]
    pl = s.get("total_pl", 0)
    pct = s.get("total_pl_pct", 0)
    sign = "+" if pl >= 0 else ""
    print(f"  Invested        : BDT {s.get('total_invested',0):,.0f}")
    print(f"  Current Value   : BDT {s.get('total_value',0):,.0f}")
    print(f"  Total P&L       : {sign}BDT {abs(pl):,.0f} ({sign}{pct:.2f}%)")
    print(f"  Holdings        : {s.get('holdings_count',0)} stocks")
    print(f"  Avg Score       : {s.get('avg_score',0):.1f}/100")

    holdings = portfolio.get("hold",[]) + portfolio.get("buy_more",[]) + portfolio.get("sell_suggestions",[])
    if holdings:
        print(f"\n  {'Ticker':<12} {'Buy':>7} {'Now':>7} {'P&L%':>7} {'Signal'}")
        print(f"  {'-'*50}")
        for h in holdings:
            pl_pct = h.get("pl_pct", 0)
            sign = "+" if pl_pct >= 0 else ""
            print(f"  {h['ticker']:<12} {h['buy_price']:>7.1f} {h['current_price']:>7.1f} {sign}{pl_pct:>6.2f}%  {h['recommendation']}")

    sell = portfolio.get("sell_suggestions", [])
    if sell:
        print(f"\n  ? SELL ALERTS:")
        for h in sell:
            print(f"    {h['ticker']}: Score={h['score']:.0f}, P&L={h['pl_pct']:.1f}%")
else:
    print("  Portfolio is empty")

# -- 7. Today's Action Plan --------------------------------------
divider("TODAY'S ACTION PLAN")
print("  1. Check stop losses on all holdings before market opens")
print("  2. DSE opens at 10:30 AM - first 30 min is volatile, avoid trading")
print("  3. Best entry window: 11:00 AM - 12:30 PM")
print("  4. Set alerts for oversold stocks if RSI crosses back above 30")
print("  5. Market closes at 2:30 PM - data auto-updates at 3:00 PM")

conn.close()
divider()
print(f"  Generated: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
print(f"  Next auto-update: Today 3:00 PM BST")
divider()

##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\backend\resume_eps_history.py #####
import requests
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
import sqlite3
import time

conn = sqlite3.connect("dse.db")
cur = conn.cursor()

def scrape_eps_history(ticker, retries=3):
    url = f"https://www.dsebd.org/displayCompany.php?name={ticker}"
    headers = {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)"}
    for attempt in range(retries):
        try:
            r = requests.get(url, headers=headers, timeout=20)
            soup = BeautifulSoup(r.text, "html.parser")
            lines = [l.strip() for l in soup.get_text(separator="\n").split("\n") if l.strip()]
            history = {}
            for i, line in enumerate(lines):
                if line in ["2021","2022","2023","2024","2025"] and i+7 < len(lines):
                    year = int(line)
                    if year in history:
                        continue
                    try:
                        eps = float(lines[i+4])
                        nav = float(lines[i+7])
                        if nav > 5:
                            history[year] = {"eps": eps, "nav": nav}
                    except:
                        try:
                            eps = float(lines[i+4])
                            nav = float(lines[i+5])
                            if nav > 5 and nav != eps:
                                history[year] = {"eps": eps, "nav": nav}
                        except:
                            pass
            return history
        except Exception as e:
            if attempt < retries-1:
                time.sleep(3)
            else:
                return {}
    return {}

def calc_trend(history):
    years = sorted(history.keys())
    if len(years) < 2:
        return 0.0
    vals = [history[y]["eps"] for y in years]
    oldest, latest = vals[0], vals[-1]
    if oldest > 0:
        return round((latest - oldest) / oldest * 100, 1)
    return 0.0

# Only process stocks that don't have history yet
cur.execute("""SELECT s.ticker FROM stocks s
LEFT JOIN fundamentals f ON s.ticker=f.ticker
WHERE f.eps_2025 IS NULL OR f.eps_2024 IS NULL
ORDER BY s.ticker""")
tickers = [r[0] for r in cur.fetchall()]
print(f"Resuming - {len(tickers)} stocks still need history...")

success = 0
for i, ticker in enumerate(tickers):
    history = scrape_eps_history(ticker)
    if history:
        trend = calc_trend(history)
        cur.execute("""UPDATE fundamentals SET
            eps_2021=?, eps_2022=?, eps_2023=?, eps_2024=?, eps_2025=?,
            nav_2021=?, nav_2022=?, nav_2023=?, nav_2024=?, nav_2025=?, eps_trend=?
            WHERE ticker=?""", (
            history.get(2021,{}).get("eps"), history.get(2022,{}).get("eps"),
            history.get(2023,{}).get("eps"), history.get(2024,{}).get("eps"),
            history.get(2025,{}).get("eps"), history.get(2021,{}).get("nav"),
            history.get(2022,{}).get("nav"), history.get(2023,{}).get("nav"),
            history.get(2024,{}).get("nav"), history.get(2025,{}).get("nav"),
            trend, ticker))
        conn.commit()
        success += 1
    if (i+1) % 20 == 0:
        print(f"Progress: {i+1}/{len(tickers)} | Success: {success}")
    time.sleep(1)

conn.close()
print(f"Done! Loaded history for {success} more stocks")

##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\backend\scan_all.py #####
import requests
import json

API = "http://localhost:8000"

stocks = requests.get(f"{API}/stocks").json()
stock_list = stocks.get("stocks", []) if isinstance(stocks, dict) else stocks
tickers = [s["ticker"] for s in stock_list if s.get("ltp", 0) > 0]

print(f"Analyzing {len(tickers)} stocks...")

results = []
errors = 0
for i, ticker in enumerate(tickers):
    try:
        r = requests.get(f"{API}/stocks/{ticker}/analysis/technical", timeout=10)
        if r.status_code == 200:
            d = r.json()
            ind = d.get("indicators", {})
            results.append({
                "ticker": ticker,
                "score": d.get("score", 0),
                "rec": d.get("recommendation", ""),
                "rsi": ind.get("rsi", 0),
                "macd_hist": ind.get("macd_hist", 0),
                "volume_ratio": ind.get("volume_ratio", 0),
                "pct_from_52h": ind.get("pct_from_52h", 0),
                "price": ind.get("current_price", 0),
                "above_sma20": ind.get("current_price", 0) > ind.get("sma20", 999),
                "above_sma50": ind.get("current_price", 0) > ind.get("sma50", 999),
                "above_sma200": ind.get("current_price", 0) > ind.get("sma200", 999),
            })
    except:
        errors += 1
    if (i+1) % 50 == 0:
        print(f"Progress: {i+1}/{len(tickers)}")

print(f"Done. Analyzed {len(results)} stocks, {errors} errors")
results.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"], reverse=True)

with open("tech_analysis_all.json", "w") as f:
    json.dump(results, f, indent=2)
print("Saved to tech_analysis_all.json")

print("\n=== TOP 20 TECHNICAL BUY SIGNALS ===")
buys = [r for r in results if r["rec"] == "BUY"]
for r in buys[:20]:
    print(f"{r['ticker']}: Score={r['score']}, RSI={r['rsi']:.1f}, MACD={r['macd_hist']:.3f}, Vol={r['volume_ratio']:.2f}x, 52H={r['pct_from_52h']:.1f}%")

print("\n=== TOP 20 TECHNICAL SELL SIGNALS ===")
sells = [r for r in results if r["rec"] == "SELL"]
sells.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"])
for r in sells[:20]:
    print(f"{r['ticker']}: Score={r['score']}, RSI={r['rsi']:.1f}, MACD={r['macd_hist']:.3f}, Vol={r['volume_ratio']:.2f}x, 52H={r['pct_from_52h']:.1f}%")

print("\n=== OVERSOLD STOCKS RSI < 30 ===")
oversold = [r for r in results if 0 < r.get("rsi", 100) < 30]
oversold.sort(key=lambda x: x["rsi"])
for r in oversold:
    print(f"{r['ticker']}: RSI={r['rsi']:.1f}, Score={r['score']}, Price={r['price']}")

print("\n=== OVERBOUGHT STOCKS RSI > 70 ===")
overbought = [r for r in results if r.get("rsi", 0) > 70]
overbought.sort(key=lambda x: x["rsi"], reverse=True)
for r in overbought[:15]:
    print(f"{r['ticker']}: RSI={r['rsi']:.1f}, Score={r['score']}, Price={r['price']}")

##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\DSE Analyzer.bat #####
@echo off
cd /d "C:\dse analyzer\backend"
start "DSE Analyzer" python -m uvicorn app.main:app
timeout /t 3 /nobreak > nul
start "" "http://localhost:8000/dashboard"
echo DSE Analyzer started. Close this window to stop.
pause


##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\full_stock_analysis.py #####
import requests
import json
import sqlite3
import time

API = "http://localhost:8000"

# Get all stocks
stocks = requests.get(f"{API}/stocks").json()["stocks"]
print(f"Total stocks: {len(stocks)}")

conn = sqlite3.connect("dse.db")
cur = conn.cursor()

results = []
failed = 0

for i, stock in enumerate(stocks):
    ticker = stock["ticker"]
    try:
        r = requests.get(f"{API}/stocks/{ticker}/analysis", timeout=15).json()
        results.append({
            "ticker": ticker,
            "sector": stock.get("sector"),
            "ltp": stock.get("ltp"),
            "percent_change": stock.get("percent_change"),
            "volume": stock.get("volume"),
            "combined_score": r.get("combined_score"),
            "tech_score": r.get("tech_score"),
            "fund_score": r.get("fund_score"),
            "recommendation": r.get("recommendation"),
            "rsi": r.get("technical", {}).get("indicators", {}).get("rsi"),
            "macd_hist": r.get("technical", {}).get("indicators", {}).get("macd_hist"),
            "volume_ratio": r.get("technical", {}).get("indicators", {}).get("volume_ratio"),
            "pct_from_52h": r.get("technical", {}).get("indicators", {}).get("pct_from_52h"),
            "sma20": r.get("technical", {}).get("indicators", {}).get("sma20"),
            "sma50": r.get("technical", {}).get("indicators", {}).get("sma50"),
            "eps": r.get("fundamental", {}).get("eps"),
            "pe": r.get("fundamental", {}).get("pe"),
            "nav": r.get("fundamental", {}).get("nav"),
            "pb": r.get("fundamental", {}).get("pb"),
            "eps_trend": None,
            "debt_equity": None,
        })
        # Get eps_trend and D/E from DB
        cur.execute("SELECT eps_trend, total_debt_mn, shares_outstanding, nav FROM fundamentals WHERE ticker=?", (ticker,))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        if row:
            results[-1]["eps_trend"] = row[0]
            if row[1] and row[2] and row[3] and row[2] > 0 and row[3] > 0:
                equity = row[2] * row[3] / 1_000_000
                results[-1]["debt_equity"] = round(row[1] / equity, 2) if equity > 0 else None

    except Exception as e:
        failed += 1

    if (i+1) % 50 == 0:
        print(f"Progress: {i+1}/{len(stocks)} | Failed: {failed}")

conn.close()

# Save to JSON
with open("full_analysis.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(results, f, indent=2, default=str)

# Save to CSV for easy viewing
import csv
with open("full_analysis.csv", "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    if results:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=results[0].keys())
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(results)

print(f"\nDone! {len(results)} stocks analyzed, {failed} failed")
print(f"Saved to full_analysis.json and full_analysis.csv")

python -m venv venv


##### FILE: C:\dse analyzer\Swing_Project_Context.md #####
# Swing Project Context — U1254 (LankaBangla / DSE)

> **Purpose:** Standing primer for this project. A new chat should read this first to orient.
> Upload to the project's knowledge and update the dated sections as the book changes.
> **Account:** Client U1254 · RAFSAAN MUHAB SHAMS · LankaBangla Securities · CASH account (no margin).
> **Last updated from:** statement As-On 11-Jun-2026 + scan `full_analysis_8th_June.csv` (8-Jun close).
> *Everything here is mechanical strategy output, not financial advice.*

---

## 1. The system (how the pieces fit)

A FastAPI server (`app.main:app`) holds market data in `dse.db`. Three POST endpoints refresh it; `full_stock_analysis.py` then scores every stock and writes `full_analysis.json` + `full_analysis.csv`.

**Environment:** Windows, PowerShell, working dir `C:\dse analyzer\backend`. Scripts use **relative** `dse.db`, so always run them from the backend dir. In PowerShell use `cd "C:\dse analyzer\backend"` (no `/d`), and `curl.exe` / `Invoke-RestMethod` (not the `curl` alias).

**Refresh + scan commands:**
```powershell
cd "C:\dse analyzer\backend"
python -m uvicorn app.main:app --port 8000      # leave running (kill stale: taskkill /PID <pid> /F)
python auto_update.py                            # POST /load-dse, /load-fundamentals, /load-history?days=30
python full_stock_analysis.py                    # writes full_analysis.json + .csv
```
Scan columns: `ticker, sector, ltp, percent_change, volume, combined_score, tech_score, fund_score, recommendation, rsi, macd_hist, volume_ratio, pct_from_52h, sma20, sma50, eps, pe, nav, pb, eps_trend, debt_equity`.

## 2. Session workflow (repeat each trading day)

1. **User uploads the broker statement PDF** (`Portfolio_U1254.pdf`). This is **authoritative** for prices, quantities, cost basis, P/L, cash, and sector exposure.
2. **Use the matching scan CSV** for signals (score/RSI/MACD/SMA). The scan whose LTP matches the statement's market prices is the right one (statement "As-On D" ≈ prior-session close = scan of that close).
3. **Produce:** a dated status file `Swing_Portfolio_U1254_Status_YYYY-MM-DD.md` and a color-coded action-sheet PNG `Swing_U1254_Action_Sheet_YYYY-MM-DD.png` (HOLD = blue, new-buy/breakeven = green, watch/extended = amber, exit = red, scale-out = orange).
4. **Anchor prices/P&L to the statement; treat scan signals as ~1 session old** and flag it. If the user runs a fresh scan, recompute stops/overbought off it.

## 3. Strategy rules (7-day swing)

- **Entry filter (all must hold):** combined score ≥ 70 · RSI 50–68 (rising, not overbought) · MACD histogram > 0 · price above SMA20 **and** SMA50 · volume ≥ ~1.3× average · liquid (turnover ≥ ~20mn so a position can be exited) · ~2–22% below 52-week high (room to target). **RSI > 72 → no fresh entry.**
- **Position management:** 6–9 positions · no single name > ~15% of book · keep 3–4+ sectors · hold ~10–15% dry powder · hard stop just under SMA20 / last swing low (~4–5% risk per trade) · move stop to **breakeven at +3–4%**.
- **Exits:** scale out ~half at first target (~+7–10% or next resistance), trail the rest. Exit on any of: stop hit · RSI > 75 on a down day · MACD histogram flips negative · 7 sessions with no progress (time stop).
- **Concentration rule:** when a sector is overweight (esp. pharma), **new buys must come from other sectors.**
- **Two horizons, two lenses:** swing = momentum/technical (RSI, MACD, volume, SMA). Long-term = fundamentals (low P/E, low P/B, growing EPS, low debt) and ignores RSI. Don't mix the lenses.

## 4. Current holdings — cost basis (from 11-Jun statement)

PV 160,581 · cost 158,058 · unrealized **+2,523 (+1.60%)** · cash **33,629 (only ~23,669 settled)** · account equity 191,687.

## 5. Open / recurring action items

1. **PTL** — sitting on breakeven stop (~62.6); honor it if it slips.
2. **BXPHARMA** — largest position (~18%); consider trimming toward ~15% to de-risk; keep 122 stop regardless.
3. **BSRMSTEEL** — keep trailing ~76.
4. **Move new winners to breakeven** as they clear +3–4% (DBH, PTL done).
5. **Watch CONFIDCEM (stop 62.5) and ENVOYTEX (stop 48)** — red but above stops.
6. **Deploy dry powder only on a confirmed setup**, from under-weighted **non-pharma, non-textile** sectors (bank/NBFI/fuel/insurance). Don't chase a gapped-up tape.

## 6. Standing flags (carry into every session)

- **BXPHARMA court/governance overhang (LIVE).** BSEC moved to appoint 9 independent directors after Aug-2024 regime change; board can't approve accounts → audited FY-June-2025 results unpublished → London AIM GDR trading suspended from 2-Jan-2026. High Court writ hearings run after the 15-Mar–18-Apr-2026 vacation; a decision was expected **around end of June 2026** — a binary, two-sided catalyst. Business still sound (declared 40% cash dividend). DSE/CSE shares trade normally. **Its 122 stop is the most important line in the book.** Re-check for a ruling before acting near that event.
- **Pharma concentration** ~45–47% (SQUR + BX + ACME ≈ 41%). Above the comfort zone → new buys stay non-pharma.
- **Textile concentration** crept to ~16% (ENVOYTEX + MALEKSPIN + PTL) → second-largest sector, **no textile adds.**
- **Cash-settlement quirk.** DSE is T+ settlement: sale proceeds and just-bought shares aren't immediately usable. Headline cash overstates buying power (e.g. 33,629 total but only ~23,669 settled); just-bought lots show **saleable-0** until they settle and can't be sold that session. Always separate settled vs maturing cash.
- **Execution pattern to watch:** buys get executed precisely; the sell/cleanup side has historically been deferred. Push the exits.
- **Data freshness:** statement = live; scan signals lag ~1 session. After a rally, RSI/scores read more overbought than the file shows — refresh before new entries.

## 7. Useful reference (fundamentals snapshot, 8-Jun)

Top quality+value names on the exchange (cheap, profitable, growing, low-debt): MPETROLEUM, PADMAOIL, CITYBANK, SQURPHARMA, UTTARABANK, EHL. Caveats: fuel distributors are policy-driven & illiquid; banks (CITYBANK/UTTARA/ONEBANK/SHAHJA) share NPL/asset-quality risk — treat as one risk; ENVOYTEX +1,400% EPS trend is a base-effect artifact. BERGERPBL = quality business but richly valued (P/E ~19, P/B ~4.2) → long-term "wait for pullback," not a swing entry.

---

### How to start a new chat in this project
Upload the latest `Portfolio_U1254.pdf` (and a fresh `full_analysis*.csv` if you've re-scanned). Say e.g. *"New session — update the book."* The chat will read this primer for rules/flags, the statement for the live book, and the scan for signals, then produce the dated status + action-sheet image. Keep this file updated (sections 4–6) each session, or ask the chat to regenerate it.
